To support input-level risk analysis before speech generation, we propose a lightweight Text Risk Score (TRS), which estimates synthesis risk from interpretable text features without manual annotation or model training. Experiments on three representative multilingual TTS systems, including OmniVoice, VoxCPM2, and MMS-TTS, show that complex text inputs expose systematic failure patterns that are not fully reflected by ordinary short-sentence evaluation. Different systems exhibit distinct vulnerabilities in number normalization, named entity handling, long-text generation, and code-switched input processing. Furthermore, TRS shows a positive correlation with content errors and duration abnormalities, demonstrating its usefulness as a low-cost pre-synthesis indicator for complex-text risk diagnosis in low-resource multilingual TTS.

</details>


### [109] [A Training-Free, Alignment-Free Approach to Corporate Intelligence: Application to SEC Filings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11620)
*Jean-François Delpech*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无需训练、无需向量对齐的企业文本分析框架：将词语通过哈希映射到固定的高维稀疏基底，并在句子上下文中累积形成可线性组合的语义签名，用于快速文档比较、发行人识别、词汇变化追踪和主题句抽取。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金融披露分析面临上下文窗口限制、幻觉风险、较高计算成本，以及不同模型独立训练后向量空间任意旋转、难以直接比较等问题。本文旨在提供一种低成本、可解释、跨时间和跨文档保持统一坐标系的替代方案。

Method: 构建确定性的稀疏种子向量：将词字符串哈希到固定的高维基底，使所有文档和不同时间阶段天然位于同一坐标系统中，无需模型训练或后处理对齐。进一步在句子上下文中累积种子向量，生成语料库特定的语义签名，并利用线性组合支持文档相似度计算、公司指纹识别、申报文件间词汇漂移追踪和主题句提取。方法在包含10-K、10-Q和8-K的多年SEC申报语料上进行验证。

Result: 实验表明，该方法能够在普通CPU硬件上实现亚秒级文档比较，并识别出具有区分度且可解释的企业事件语义画像，包括波音737 MAX危机、英特尔供应链中断以及Bunge收购Viterra。每个语义画像都可以追溯到生成该画像的具体原句，且不依赖领域专用训练或大语言模型推理。摘要未提供定量基准、数据规模、对比方法或统计显著性结果。

Conclusion: 确定性稀疏种子向量为金融披露分析提供了一种训练免除、对齐免除、计算高效且可解释的文本表示方案。其固定坐标系和线性可组合性尤其适合跨时间企业分析与事件追踪。不过，摘要层面的证据主要是案例展示，方法相对于强基线的性能优势和泛化能力仍需通过更系统的定量实验验证。

Abstract: High-dimensional dense text embeddings and large language models face real obstacles in financial-disclosure analysis: context-window limits, hallucination risk, high computational cost, and the arbitrary rotation of vector spaces across independently trained models. We present a training-free, alignment-free framework for corporate intelligence built on deterministic sparse seed vectors. Hashing word strings into a fixed high-dimensional basis places all documents and all temporal epochs in a common coordinate system by construction, removing any need for training or alignment. Accumulating these seed vectors across sentence contexts yields corpus-specific semantic signatures that compose linearly, supporting sub-second document comparison, issuer fingerprinting, tracking of how an issuer's vocabulary shifts between filings, and thematic sentence extraction, all on ordinary CPU hardware. Demonstrating the approach on a multi-year corpus of SEC filings (10-K, 10-Q, 8-K), we show how material corporate events, among them Boeing's 737 MAX crisis, Intel's supply-chain disruptions, and Bunge's acquisition of Viterra, emerge as distinct, interpretable semantic profiles, each traceable to the exact source sentences that produced it, with no domain-specific training and no LLM inference.

</details>


### [110] [RAG-Safety-Bench: Reliable Evaluation of Retrieval-Augmented LLM Safety](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11758)
*Adithiyan Rajan Indira Saravanan,Kathleen C. Fraser*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 RAG-Safety-Bench，用于系统评估检索增强生成（RAG）对大语言模型安全性的影响。通过控制文档类型并排除检索器质量这一混杂因素，研究发现：RAG 可能削弱模型原有的安全防护；模型的有益能力与不安全能力存在反向关系；即使检索文档本身安全，也可能诱发不安全生成，且影响具有模型特异性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究表明，RAG 虽能提升信息可靠性并减少幻觉，但在处理有害请求时可能产生安全副作用。随着企业将内部文档和知识库接入 LLM 系统，有必要明确 RAG 导致安全性能下降的机制，并区分检索质量、文档内容和模型自身能力等因素的影响。

Method: 提出 RAG-Safety-Bench，并在五个开源 LLM 上进行评测。实验设置包含四种条件：非 RAG；检索包含有害请求答案的相关文档；检索与请求相关但不含具体答案的文档；检索随机且安全的文档。该设计通过固定或消除检索器质量的影响，分离不同文档因素对安全性的作用。

Result: 结果显示：1）模型的良性能力与不安全能力呈反向关系；2）模型在基础条件下具备的安全防护，不能保证其在 RAG 场景下仍然有效；3）部分模型即使接收到无害文档，也会生成更不安全的内容；4）安全退化现象及其程度具有明显的模型特异性。

Conclusion: RAG 并非天然提升系统安全性，甚至可能绕过或削弱 LLM 的既有安全护栏。部署 RAG 系统时，不能仅依赖基础模型的安全评测或文档安全性，还应开展面向检索条件、模型差异和端到端生成行为的专门安全测试。

Abstract: Allowing large language models (LLMs) to retrieve information from a set of trusted documents can increase reliability and reduce hallucination. However, recent work has demonstrated that retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) can have unintended side effects on the overall safety of the generated responses, when prompted for harmful or dangerous content. A clearer understanding of the mechanisms leading to this result is needed, as increasing numbers of end users turn to RAG to incorporate corporate documents and knowledge bases into LLM-based systems. We introduce RAG-Safety-Bench, a benchmark to measure the safety impact of RAG on LLM models. By removing the confounding effect of retriever quality, and cleanly separating the problem into four conditions -- non-RAG, RAG with an oracle document containing the answer to the harmful request, RAG with documents related to the harmful request but without the specific answer, and RAG with random, safe documents -- the benchmark isolates the impacts of different factors in the observed safety degradation. We report results across five open-source LLMs, showing an inverse relationship between benign and unsafe capability, strong evidence that baseline safety guardrails do not lead to downstream safety guarantees in the RAG case, and model-specific support for previous findings that even benign documents can lead to unsafe generation in retrieval-enabled systems.

</details>


### [111] [Component-Aware Differential Privacy for Federated Multilingual Speech-LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11762)
*Jordi Luque,Fernando López,Aleix Sant*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究语音大语言模型联邦学习中的分层差分隐私裁剪失效问题，并提出α-split双池分配方法，在保持整体DP保证不变的同时，改善WER效用并增强编码器的噪声保护。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有按参数量分配分层裁剪预算的方法通常能提升梯度保真度，但语音LLM的声学编码器与语言解码器更新范数可能相差一个数量级，导致单池分层方法发生跨组件预算坍缩，使词错误率显著恶化，甚至训练失败。因此需要识别失效机制并设计适配不同组件更新尺度的隐私预算分配方案。

Method: 作者在三种语音LLM架构上评估六种分层方法，分析编码器与语言模型组件间的更新范数不平衡及其对性能的影响。进一步提出α-split：将编码器参数和LLM参数划分为两个独立的裁剪与噪声分配池，并通过架构校准α确定两池的分配比例。同时证明该方法的联合L2敏感度和原始(ε,δ)-差分隐私保证保持不变。

Result: 实验显示，单池分层方法在组件更新范数差异较大时会出现跨组件预算坍缩，WER可能远离平坦全局裁剪的结果，甚至导致训练崩溃；当范数不平衡较弱时，自适应单池方法只能部分恢复性能，说明坍缩程度随组件间范数比增大而加剧。采用架构校准的α后，α-split恢复了相对于平坦DP的WER效用；同时，编码器针对基于说话人语音的梯度反演攻击获得4.47倍更强的单组件噪声保护，而LLM噪声开销仅增加2.6%。

Conclusion: 语音LLM中的显著组件级更新范数不平衡会破坏传统单池分层DP裁剪的预算分配。将不同组件独立归一化并分池处理能够避免跨组件预算坍缩，在不改变整体隐私保证的前提下兼顾模型效用与组件级隐私保护，说明分层DP设计应考虑模型架构和组件尺度差异。

Abstract: Per-layer differential privacy (DP) clipping improves gradient fidelity in federated learning by allocating per-matrix clipping budgets proportional to parameter count. We show that this recipe breaks for speech large language models (speech-LLMs), when the acoustic encoder and the language decoder differ by an order of magnitude in update norm. Single-pool per-layer methods suffer \emph{cross-component budget collapse}, dragging word error rate (WER) far from flat global clipping or collapsing training entirely. When the norm imbalance is milder, adaptive single-pool methods partially recover, confirming that collapse severity scales with the inter-component norm ratio. We empirically diagnose the root cause across six per-layer methods and three speech-LLM architectures. We then propose \emph{$α$-split}, a two-pool allocation that normalises encoder and LLM parameters into independent pools, and show that joint $\ell_2$ sensitivity and the original $(\varepsilon,δ)$-DP guarantee are unchanged. At architecture-calibrated $α$, our method recovers WER utility compared to flat DP, while granting the encoder $4.47{\times}$ tighter per-component noise protection against speaker voice-based gradient-inversion attacks at only $+2.6\%$ LLM noise overhead.

</details>


### [112] [Recognizing Is Not Reversing: A Controlled Inversion Test of Fact-Preserving News Framing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11769)
*Yi Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种受控的新闻框架逆转测试，检验大语言模型能否在保持事实不变的前提下，撤销已知的文本框架干预。结果显示，模型事实保真度较高，但框架逆转能力极弱，说明框架识别、事实保持与框架逆转是彼此分离的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有新闻框架研究主要关注文本生成、框架检测，或改写结果是否显得更中性，缺少对模型能否撤销已知框架变换的直接评估。本文旨在填补这一空白，并区分事实保真、框架识别和框架逆转这三种能力。

Method: 研究围绕三种已有的文本框架实现方式开展受控逆转测试：评价性词汇、施事关系表达和信息显著性。基于60篇新闻文章，设置三种干预强度，构造540组成对变体；所有变体保留原子事实，并记录具体编辑操作。随后在Qwen、DeepSeek和Kimi等模型上评估事实保持、框架类型与方向识别，以及框架逆转效果。

Result: 各模型的事实保持率约为0.84，表明其大体能够维持文章事实。但框架逆转率仅为0.044—0.068，即使模型同时正确识别框架类型和干预方向，汇总后的逆转率也只有0.071。这说明模型虽然可能识别文本如何被框架化，却很少能够准确撤销该框架。

Conclusion: 本文证明，事实保真度高并不等于具备框架逆转能力，框架识别也不能自然推导出框架消除能力。受控逆转测试可作为评估新闻改写模型的重要补充，有助于更严格地检验模型在保持事实的同时处理立场、突出程度和语言评价等框架因素的能力。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used to analyze and rewrite news, yet current framing studies mainly evaluate generation, detection, or whether rewritten text appears more neutral. They do not directly show whether a model can undo a known framing transformation while keeping the facts fixed. We introduce a controlled inversion test over three established textual realizations of framing: evaluative lexis, agency realization, and information salience. Across 60 news articles and three intervention strengths, this yields 540 paired variants with preserved atomic facts and recorded edits. Across Qwen, DeepSeek, and Kimi, factual preservation remains near 0.84, whereas intervention reversal is 0.044--0.068. Even when both framing type and direction are recognized correctly, pooled reversal reaches 0.071. These results reveal a clear separation between factual fidelity, framing recognition, and framing inversion: recognizing how an article is framed does not imply that the framing can be undone.

</details>


### [113] [The widening evaluation gap in medical large language model research 2023 to 2026](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11770)
*Raad Bin Tareaf,Murad Al-Rajab,Samia Loucif*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究系统分析2023年1月至2026年6月医学领域大语言模型研究的数量、研究设计、模型新旧程度与发表滞后，发现医学研究规模快速增长，但严谨性较高的研究往往评估已过时或停用的模型；模型迁移只能部分抵消模型老化，研究设计与模型时效性之间存在结构性张力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型迭代速度以季度计，而临床证据生产通常需要数年。研究者希望判断医学研究是否能够及时跟上被评估模型的发展，并明确研究设计、模型选择和评估滞后之间的关系。

Method: 检索PubMed中2023年1月至2026年6月、涉及14个临床领域的相关记录，共获得11,628篇。统计研究数量增长、随机/对照/前瞻性设计比例，以及从研究所指向的最新模型版本到论文发表之间的评估滞后。进一步构建“模型组成固定”的反事实基准，以区分模型自然老化与研究迁移至新模型的影响；比较不同研究设计评估模型的年龄，并分析仍在开发模型与已停用模型的使用情况。

Result: 相关文献数量增长约45倍，但仅2.5%采用随机、对照或前瞻性设计。评估滞后从1.33个季度扩大到6.08个季度。迁移至更新系统仅抵消模型老化漂移的56%（95% CI：50%–65%）。随机试验评估的模型中位数比其他设计老4.6个季度，差异显著（P=3×10^-19）；但在仅分析仍在开发模型的研究后，不同研究设计之间不再存在差异。62%的随机试验评估的是已停用模型家族。

Conclusion: 医学大语言模型研究在数量上迅速扩张，但模型时效性明显落后。严谨研究与模型新颖性之间的矛盾主要源于模型选择，而非单纯由研究流程或发表周期造成。未来应在研究设计、模型注册与版本追踪中同时纳入证据严谨性和模型时效性。

Abstract: Large language models are superseded every few quarters; clinical evidence takes years. We asked whether medical research is keeping pace with the systems it evaluates. PubMed returned 11,628 records for January 2023 to June 2026 across fourteen clinical domains, growing 45-fold; 2.5% used a randomised, controlled or prospective design. Evaluation lag, from a study's newest named model release to its own publication, widened from 1.33 to 6.08 quarters. Because discontinued models age mechanically, we benchmarked this against a counterfactual holding model composition fixed: migration to newer systems offset only 56% of the drift (95% CI 50-65). Randomised trials evaluated models a median 4.6 quarters older than other designs (P = 3 x 10^-19), yet among studies naming a model still under development no design differed from any other; 62% of randomised trials evaluated a discontinued family. Rigour and currency are in tension, and that tension reflects model selection rather than research timelines.

</details>


### [114] [Beyond Word Error Rate: A Switch Aware Evaluation of ASR and Audio Language Models on English Yoruba Code-Switched Speech](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11786)
*Chibuzor Okocha,Christan Earl Grant*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出面向英语—约鲁巴语码转换语音的切换感知评测框架，比较六个ASR模型和五个音频语言模型。结果表明，整体WER会掩盖码转换处的性能差异：部分音频语言模型在切换局部指标上优于最佳ASR，但约鲁巴语识别在几乎所有系统中严重崩溃，且生成式音频语言模型常出现翻译、冗余输出和提示泄漏。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ASR和音频语言模型通常在单语基准上取得较低错误率，但对低资源、富含变音符号语言中的语码转换语音缺乏系统了解。单一的整体词错误率（WER）无法揭示模型在语言切换位置、不同语言片段以及变音符号处理上的具体弱点，因此需要可复现且更细粒度的评测方法。

Method: 作者构建了一个确定性的2000条英语—约鲁巴语语码转换语音评测集，并使用统一评分流程评估11个现代系统，包括6个ASR模型和5个音频语言模型。除整体WER外，还报告切换进入词元错误率（SETER）、切换点窗口错误率、分语言错误率，以及忽略变音符号后的WER；同时分析生成式音频语言模型的非转录行为及其对提示词的依赖。作者还发布数据清单、指标实现和评测脚本。

Result: 整体WER不能准确反映语码转换能力。某ASR模型虽取得最佳WER，但与领先音频语言模型在WER上无显著差异；后者却在所有切换局部指标上显著更优。对能够忠实转录的系统而言，约鲁巴语词元识别错误率几乎普遍达到0.97，而英语识别明显更好；错误尤其集中在切换进入约鲁巴语的位置。若干生成式音频语言模型无法进行严格转录，表现为翻译、过度冗长和提示泄漏，且这些行为显著依赖提示词。

Conclusion: 语码转换语音评测不能只依赖整体WER，应结合切换局部、分语言和变音符号鲁棒性指标。当前系统对低资源约鲁巴语及切换边界存在严重不平衡，生成式音频语言模型还可能偏离转录任务定义。该工作为非洲语码转换语音提供了可复现的基准和工具，也表明未来模型需要增强低资源语言建模、切换边界处理及输出约束能力。

Abstract: Automatic speech recognition (ASR) systems and audio language models (audio LMs) now report low error rates on monolingual benchmarks, but their behavior on code switched speech in low resource, diacritic rich languages remains poorly characterized. We present a switch aware evaluation of eleven modern systems (six ASR models and five audio LMs) on English Yoruba code-switched speech, using a deterministic 2000 utterance evaluation set and a shared scoring pipeline. Beyond word error rate (WER), we report switch localized diagnostics: a switch entry token error rate (SETER), windowed switch point error rates, language specific error rates, and a diacritic insensitive WER. Our central finding is that aggregate WER hides code switching behavior. The best system by WER (an ASR model) is statistically indistinguishable from a leading audio LM on WER, yet the audio LM is significantly better on every switch localized metric. Across faithful systems, Yoruba token recognition collapses (error 0.97 for almost all systems) while English tokens are recognized far better, and errors concentrate sharply at switches into Yoruba. Several generative audio LMs fail as exact transcribers, producing translation, verbosity, and prompt leakage that are strongly prompt dependent. We release manifests, metric implementations, and evaluation scripts to support reproducible, switch aware benchmarking for African code switched speech.

</details>


### [115] [Target leakage, not model class, explains reported accuracy in survey-based cardiovascular screening: a leakage-tiered audit of glass-box and tabular foundation models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11838)
*Raad Bin Tareaf,Murad Al-Rajab,Samia Loucif,Samer Ellaham,Cedric Schmitz*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究表明，心血管筛查模型中接近0.89的AUROC主要来自特征泄漏，而非复杂模型本身；去除两个诊断后特征后，各类模型性能几乎收敛。可解释模型在性能、速度、校准、公平性、覆盖率和可审计性之间表现出更优的部署价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有国家健康调查中的心血管筛查模型常报告约0.89的AUROC，但这一高准确率可能源于诊断后信息泄漏，而不是模型真正学习到了可部署的风险模式。此外，尚不清楚表格基础模型能否改变这一结论，以及模型部署所需的校准、公平性、不确定性、解释性和推理成本能否同时满足。

Method: 研究使用2022年美国行为风险因素监测系统（BRFSS）的442,067名受访者，针对既往心肌梗死患病状态，比较10种分类器，覆盖线性模型、树集成、神经网络、玻璃盒模型和表格基础模型。模型在五种泄漏风险递减的特征层级上进行评测，并审计AUROC、校准、明确筛查阈值下的公平性、保序/保形预测覆盖率、解释忠实度和推理成本。随后将模型与阈值冻结，并外推至2023年BRFSS的430,755名受访者。研究还通过编辑可解释增强模型的形状函数进行公平性修正，并比较边际校准与Mondrian分层校准。

Result: 删除两个诊断后特征后，所有模型的AUROC均下降0.049至0.051，且性能收敛在仅0.0045的范围内，说明高性能主要由特征集合而非学习器造成。可解释增强模型在预设0.005非劣效界限内不劣于所有其他模型，同时推理速度约为最强表格基础模型的104倍。固定阈值下，女性心肌梗死检出率为75.4%，男性为89.0%；编辑形状函数后，性别差距降至0.010。边际保形预测在男性中的覆盖率为0.86、在60岁以上成人中的覆盖率为0.82，而Mondrian校准修复了所有分层覆盖率问题。冻结模型在2023年数据上的AUROC变化不超过0.002。

Conclusion: 该研究认为，相关文献中的性能上限主要是特征泄漏造成的，而不是模型容量不足。透明的玻璃盒模型没有带来可测量的性能损失，却使公平性修正和不确定性条件化能够被直接审计。对于临床筛查部署，严格的特征设计、外部时间验证、分层校准和多维度评估比追求更复杂的模型更重要。

Abstract: Cardiovascular screening models trained on national health surveys routinely report areas under the receiver operating characteristic curve (AUROC) near 0.89. We asked whether that accuracy reflects learning or target leakage, whether tabular foundation models change the answer, and whether the properties deployment requires survive joint examination. We benchmarked ten classifiers spanning linear, tree-ensemble, neural, glass-box, and tabular foundation classes for prevalent myocardial infarction in 442,067 respondents of the 2022 Behavioral Risk Factor Surveillance System across five feature tiers of decreasing leakage risk. Each was audited for discrimination, calibration, fairness at an explicit screening threshold, conformal coverage, explanation faithfulness, and inference cost, then applied -- models and thresholds frozen -- to 430,755 respondents of 2023. Removing two post-diagnostic features cost every model 0.049-0.051 AUROC, collapsing the field into a 0.0045-wide band. The glass-box explainable boosting machine was non-inferior to every alternative within a pre-specified 0.005 margin while scoring the cohort roughly 104 times faster than the strongest foundation model. One threshold detected 75.4% of women's infarctions against 89.0% of men's; editing the model's shape functions reduced the gap to 0.010. Marginal conformal prediction gave 0.86 coverage to men and 0.82 to adults over 60; Mondrian calibration repaired every stratum. Frozen models transported within 0.002 AUROC. Reported headroom in this literature is a property of the feature set, not the learner. Transparency cost nothing measurable and made fairness repair and uncertainty conditioning directly auditable. Evaluation practice, not model capacity, is the binding constraint.

</details>


### [116] [IndicTriMix: Developing Language Identification Datasets and Models for Tri-Language Code-Mixing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11851)
*Pruthwik Mishra,Rudra Trivedi,Avi Patel,Ashok Urlana,Shrikant Malviya*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究社交媒体代码混合文本中的词元级语言识别，将任务建模为序列标注，并微调MuRIL和XLM-RoBERTa，在印地语、古吉拉特语和孟加拉语数据配置上进行评估，同时发布人工标注测试集、代码混合文本生成方法及微调模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社交媒体用户经常在同一话语中切换多种语言，使代码混合文本中的词元级语言识别成为必要任务。传统面向单语文本的语言识别模型难以准确处理词元级、多语言混合场景，因此需要利用上下文信息的模型和相应基准数据集。

Method: 将词元级语言识别形式化为序列标注问题，微调适用于印度语言的上下文Transformer模型MuRIL与XLM-RoBERTa。针对印地语、古吉拉特语和孟加拉语构建三种数据配置进行实验；利用三种语言的平行句提出两种代码混合文本生成方法，并建立人工标注测试集。

Result: 实验表明，基于上下文嵌入的MuRIL和XLM-RoBERTa能够有效完成多语言社交媒体文本中的词元级语言识别。论文还发布了代码混合词元语言识别基准、人工标注测试集以及微调模型，以支持复现和后续研究。摘要未提供具体数据集规模、评价指标或数值结果，因此无法判断两种模型及不同语言配置之间的定量优劣。

Conclusion: 上下文Transformer表示适合处理代码混合文本中的词元级语言识别，能够弥补传统单语语言识别模型在多语言切换场景中的不足。该研究的主要贡献包括任务建模、跨三种印度语言的评测、两种代码混合生成方法，以及公开基准数据和模型。

Abstract: Language identification in code-mixed text, largely observed in social media, is highly essential when users frequently switch between multiple languages within a single utterance. Accurately identifying the languages of code-mixed tokens becomes an urgent necessity. Traditional language identification models, designed for monolingual text, are not well suited for token-level language identification in code-mixed settings. We formulate the task as a sequence labeling problem and fine-tune contextual transformer-based models MuRIL and XLM-RoBERTa best suited for Indian languages. We evaluate these systems on three different data configurations (Hindi, Gujarati, and Bengali) to predict language labels for individual tokens. We release a benchmark for language identification in code-mixed tokens with manually annotated test sets. We propose two approaches of code-mixed generation using parallel sentences of three languages. The trained models demonstrate the effectiveness of contextual embeddings for token-level language identification in multilingual social media text. For reproducibility and to facilitate future research, we publicly release our fine-tuned models.

</details>


### [117] [LOCUS: Task-Aware Low-Rank Post-Training for Token-Efficient Language Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11739)
*Dongfang Zhao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出 LOCUS：通过选择任务感知的低秩适配子空间，在保持偏好对齐效用约束的同时降低大语言模型生成长度与服务成本。该方法仅更新约 0.24%–0.28% 的参数，在 Pythia-2.8B 上最多减少 39.84% 的续写长度，在 Qwen2.5-3B 上减少 14.87%–17.58%，且内部偏好诊断基本不变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型的服务成本通常随输出序列长度近似线性增长，但标准偏好对齐方法可能使回答变得更冗长，却未必提升实际效用。论文关注一个被忽视的问题：后训练更新的参数化方式是否会影响生成长度，并希望在不牺牲偏好对齐效用的前提下降低输出 token 数和推理成本。

Method: 提出 LOCUS（任务感知低秩适配子空间选择方法）。首先选择与任务相关的低秩更新子空间，使模型能够针对输出长度进行有效调节；随后在冻结主干模型的条件下，仅在该子空间内进行后训练，并保留原有的偏好优化目标。实验使用 Anthropic HH-RLHF 对话偏好数据，在 Pythia-2.8B 和 Qwen2.5-3B 两个约 3B 参数的解码器模型上进行评估，并与协议匹配的全参数 DPO、DrDPO 分支及公开的 SamPO 检查点比较。

Result: LOCUS 在 Pythia-2.8B 上最多将续写长度降低 39.84%，在 Qwen2.5-3B 上降低 14.87%–17.58%；仅更新模型 0.24%–0.28% 的参数。与此同时，内部偏好诊断没有发生实质性变化，说明输出缩短并非以明显损害偏好对齐表现为代价。

Conclusion: 后训练更新的参数化方式本身可以成为控制生成长度和服务成本的有效手段。通过任务感知的低秩子空间，LOCUS 能在冻结大部分模型参数、保留原偏好目标的情况下显著减少输出长度，并维持相近的偏好对齐信号，表明低参数、高成本效率的长度控制具有可行性。

Abstract: Large language model serving costs scale directly with output sequence length, yet standard preference alignment often inflates response verbosity without improving utility. We study whether the parameterization of post-training updates affects generation length: low-rank subspaces alter sequence length without modifying the alignment loss. We present LOCUS, a method that selects a task-aware low-rank adaptation subspace to minimize output-token cost subject to a utility constraint. Within this subspace, post-training retains the native preference objective with a frozen backbone. Across Anthropic HH-RLHF dialogue preferences, we evaluate two $\sim$3B decoder backbones, Pythia-2.8B and Qwen2.5-3B, against protocol-matched full-parameter DPO and DrDPO branches and the released SamPO checkpoint. LOCUS reduces continuation length by up to 39.84\% on Pythia-2.8B and by 14.87--17.58\% on Qwen2.5-3B while updating only 0.24--0.28\% of model parameters, with no material change in the internal preference diagnostic.

</details>


### [118] [Domain-Specific Hallucination Detection in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11878)
*Varun Teja Chundru,Debasmita Biswas*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一个结合DeBERTa-v3分类、MC Dropout不确定性估计和温度缩放校准的多信号幻觉检测流程，并进一步使用DPO降低生成模型的幻觉率，同时评估跨领域迁移与领域适配效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型虽然能够生成流畅文本，但可能包含与事实或上下文不一致的非忠实陈述。现有检测方法在不确定性量化、校准、跨任务泛化和跨领域迁移方面仍存在不足，因此需要构建更可靠的响应级幻觉检测与缓解方案。

Method: 在HaluEval上训练DeBERTa-v3分类器，并结合MC Dropout进行不确定性量化、温度缩放进行概率校准；通过上下文消融和学习曲线分析检验推理依据与数据效率。随后对Qwen2.5-0.5B应用直接偏好优化（DPO）以减少幻觉。最后在SciFact生物医学基准上进行跨领域评估，并比较通用模型迁移与PubMedBERT领域特定微调的效果。

Result: 通用领域任务上，流程取得F1=0.915、AUROC=0.977；QA、摘要和对话任务的F1分别为0.97、0.96和0.82。MC Dropout推理后的准确率达到93.2%。移除知识上下文后，摘要F1下降24%，支持模型进行了实质性的蕴含推理；仅使用25%的训练数据即可达到完整数据性能的77%。DPO将Qwen2.5-0.5B的幻觉率从85.5%降至37.7%，相对下降55.9%。在SciFact上，通用领域模型迁移的F1仅为0.52；PubMedBERT领域微调后达到F1=0.63、AUROC=0.81。

Conclusion: 多信号检测能够在通用领域有效识别响应级幻觉，MC Dropout和校准机制进一步增强了可靠性；DPO可显著降低生成模型的幻觉率。但通用领域知识向生物医学领域迁移效果有限，领域匹配的预训练与微调是更有效的适配策略。

Abstract: Large language models generate fluent text that can contain unfaithful claims -- a phenomenon known as hallucination. We present a multi-signal detection pipeline combining fine-tuned DeBERTa-v3 classification, Monte Carlo (MC) Dropout uncertainty quantification, and temperature-scaled calibration for response-level hallucination detection. Evaluated on the HaluEval benchmark, our pipeline achieves F1=0.915 and AUROC=0.977 on general-domain tasks, with per-task F1 scores of 0.97 (QA), 0.96 (Summarization), and 0.82 (Dialogue). MC Dropout inference further improves accuracy to 93.2%. A context ablation study confirms the model performs genuine entailment reasoning rather than exploiting surface patterns, with summarization F1 dropping 24% when knowledge context is removed. Learning curve analysis reveals that 25% of training data captures 77% of full-data performance. Beyond detection, we apply Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) to a Qwen2.5-0.5B generator, reducing its hallucination rate from 85.5% to 37.7% (55.9% relative reduction) as measured by our detector. Cross-domain evaluation on the SciFact biomedical benchmark shows that general-domain training transfers poorly (F1=0.52), motivating domain-specific fine-tuning. PubMedBERT fine-tuned on SciFact achieves F1=0.63 and AUROC=0.81, demonstrating that domain-matched pre-training is the strongest adaptation strategy. Code and models are available at https://github.com/varunteja99/hallucination-detection-nlp

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [119] [Probabilistic Focal Search: Accelerating Bounded-Suboptimal Search via Lower-Bound Advancement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10584)
*Minh Vu Duc,Trung Le Huu,Hà Minh Hoàng,Trung Thanh Nguyen,Phuong Khanh Nguyen,Huynh Thi Thanh Binh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出概率化焦点搜索（PFS）：以概率p遵循Focal Search的启发式选择，以概率1-p选择OPEN中最小f值节点，从而推动f_min前进并更早扩大FOCAL。在N-Puzzle、TSP等存在长时间f_min平台的任务上，PFS可显著减少节点扩展；其任意版本APFS在GCTSP上表现最佳。该机制也迁移到Dynamic Potential Search，形成PDPS，但收益依赖领域和搜索界限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统Focal Search虽然能在w倍最优代价范围内寻找解，但其确定性的FOCAL选择策略可能长期不扩展最小f值节点，使f_min停滞，进而延迟新节点进入FOCAL，增加获得有界次优解所需的搜索时间。论文旨在通过平衡启发式引导与下界推进，缓解FOCAL准入延迟这一瓶颈。

Method: 提出Probabilistic Focal Search（PFS）。每次扩展时，以概率p采用标准FS在FOCAL中的引导选择，以概率1-p选择OPEN中具有最小f值的节点，促进f_min推进、扩大FOCAL并引入潜在可行解节点。进一步将相同调度机制应用于Dynamic Potential Search，得到Probabilistic Dynamic Potential Search（PDPS）；同时构造任意版本Anytime Probabilistic Focal Search（APFS）。实验在N-Puzzle、Pancake Sorting、TSP及GCTSP上进行，并测试多个w和p参数。

Result: PFS相较FS在多个基准上取得明显收益，尤其是在长时间f_min平台阻碍FOCAL有效扩大的场景中，N-Puzzle和TSP的节点扩展量最多可减少约90%或更多。APFS在GCTSP的任意算法评估中优于所有被测试方法。Pancake Sorting中的收益较小，说明当确定性搜索已能有效推进f_min时，概率机制的附加价值有限。PDPS实验表明，该思想也适用于潜势函数引导，但共同成功效果受问题领域和有界参数影响。

Conclusion: 概率化地混合FOCAL引导选择与最小f值选择，能够有效缓解FOCAL准入滞后，并在搜索进展受f_min平台限制时显著提升有界次优搜索及任意搜索性能。该方法并非普遍稳定增益，其效果取决于确定性基线是否存在下界推进瓶颈；因此，概率因子应针对具体领域和搜索界限进行调节。

Abstract: Bounded-suboptimal search seeks a solution within a factor $w$ of optimal while reducing search effort. Focal Search (FS) uses heuristic guidance within FOCAL, the frontier nodes eligible under the threshold $w f_{\min}$, but its deterministic policy may leave $f_{\min}$ unchanged for many expansions. We introduce Probabilistic Focal Search (PFS), which follows the FS guided choice with probability $p$ and expands a minimum-$f$ OPEN node with probability $1-p$. The latter branch encourages the lower bound to advance, enlarging FOCAL and admitting nodes that may lead to feasible solutions. By balancing guidance and lower-bound advancement, this mechanism can reduce time to a bounded solution when progress is limited by delayed FOCAL admission. As a secondary transfer experiment, we apply the same scheduler to Dynamic Potential Search, yielding Probabilistic Dynamic Potential Search (PDPS). We benchmark PFS against FS on N-Puzzle, Pancake Sorting, and the Traveling Salesperson Problem (TSP), and evaluate its anytime extension on the Generalized Covering TSP (GCTSP), using multiple $w$ and $p$ values. Across these benchmarks, the largest gains occur when long $f_{\min}$ plateaus delay useful FOCAL admissions; in such settings, the probabilistic factor may reduce node expansions by about 90\% or more (e.g., on N-Puzzle and TSP). For the anytime algorithm family, Anytime Probabilistic Focal Search (APFS) outperforms all tested algorithms in evaluating anytime methods on GCTSP. We also observe that the benefit is smaller when the deterministic search already advances efficiently (e.g., Pancake Sorting), indicating that the probabilistic factor is most useful when FOCAL admission is a search bottleneck. The PDPS transfer shows that the mechanism also transfers to potential guidance, although its common-success effects remain domain- and bound-dependent.

</details>


### [120] [Automating Quadratic Unconstrained Binary Optimization (QUBO) Formulation Generation from Natural Language](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10629)
*Niloy Kumar Mondal,Md Rizwan Parvez*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个端到端多智能体框架，将自然语言中的组合优化问题自动转换为QUBO formulation，并构建QUBOBench基准进行评测。框架在100个、覆盖12个应用领域的问题上达到68%的准确率，比直接单次调用基线高22个百分点；消融分析表明，迭代式自修复是性能提升的关键因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: QUBO是组合优化的重要统一建模形式，适用于量子、量子-经典混合及量子启发式求解器。但从自然语言描述生成正确QUBO需要同时识别二元变量、目标函数、约束、惩罚项及惩罚权重，流程复杂、耗时且依赖领域专家。因此，论文旨在降低QUBO建模门槛，并提高自动生成结果的正确性与可复用性。

Method: 论文提出端到端多智能体框架，依据自然语言问题描述及结构化或非结构化测试用例，自动生成QUBO formulation。框架应包含问题解析、变量与约束识别、目标及惩罚项构造、结果验证和迭代式自修复等协作过程。为系统评估方法，作者构建QUBOBench：收集100个组合优化问题，覆盖12个应用领域，来源包括同行评议文献、竞赛题目及经典NP-hard问题。实验将该框架与直接单次调用的基线进行比较。

Result: 在QUBOBench上，多智能体框架取得68%的准确率，相比直接单次调用基线提升22个百分点，说明多阶段协作和验证机制能够显著改善自然语言到QUBO的转换质量。进一步分析显示，迭代式自修复是贡献最大的组件。数据与代码已开源。摘要未说明准确率的严格判定标准、各领域分项结果、统计显著性及不同智能体配置的详细效果。

Conclusion: 该研究证明，多智能体协作结合迭代式自修复能够有效提升自动QUBO建模性能，为自然语言驱动的组合优化建模提供了可行方案。QUBOBench也为后续研究提供了统一评测基础。不过，68%的准确率仍表明系统存在较大改进空间，尤其需要进一步明确评测标准、增强复杂约束和惩罚权重的可靠处理，并验证框架在更大规模及未见问题类型上的泛化能力。

Abstract: Quadratic Unconstrained Binary Optimization (QUBO) is a central formulation for combinatorial optimization and has gained increasing attention due to its compatibility with quantum, hybrid quantum-classical, and quantum-inspired solvers. However, translating natural-language problem descriptions into correct QUBO formulations remains difficult, requiring the identification of binary variables, constraints, objective functions, penalty terms, and suitable penalty weights. This process is time-consuming and often demands substantial domain expertise. To address this challenge, we propose an end-to-end multi-agent framework that automatically generates QUBO formulations from natural-language problem descriptions, supported by structured or unstructured test cases. To evaluate its performance, We also introduce QUBOBench, a benchmark containing 100 combinatorial optimization problems across 12 application domains, curated from peer-reviewed literature, competitions, and canonical NP-hard problems. Experimental results show that our framework achieves 68% accuracy on QUBOBench, outperforming a direct single-call baseline by 22%. Further analysis identifies iterative self-repair as the most important component contributing to improved performance. The data and code are open-sourced at https://quitttcat.github.io/QuantumQUBOAgent.

</details>


### [121] [A Multi-Stage Rule-Chaining Framework for Compositional and Interpretable Cognitive Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10654)
*Deblina Kar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向ARC抽象推理任务的多阶段规则链框架，融合确定性规则发现、模式组合和结构抽象三个求解器，通过渐进式回退与推理痕迹复用，实现符号、结构和概念层面的组合推理。摘要声称该系统在ARC-AGI及ARC-AGI-2任务上取得超过95%的总体准确率，体现出较强的规则泛化、可解释性与层次化抽象能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ARC旨在测试系统能否从少量示例中归纳抽象规则并迁移应用，而非依赖大规模训练或任务特定调参。现有方法在确定性变换、组合模式和复杂层次关系之间往往缺乏统一的推理机制，因此本文旨在构建一种可解释、可组合、接近人类抽象过程的机器推理架构。

Method: 框架采用多阶段规则链和渐进式回退层级。第一阶段为确定性规则发现模块，通过几何、颜色和对象分析诱导原子变换；第二阶段为模式组合引擎，利用块合并、重复及空间启发式重建输出；第三阶段为结构抽象层，用于推断网格中的层级与嵌套关系。各求解器按顺序运行，后续阶段复用先前阶段的推理轨迹，以增强可解释性和泛化能力。

Result: 摘要报告系统在训练任务中通过995/1000，在进一步评估中通过105/120，并在240个ARC-AGI-2测试任务中解决230个，声称总体准确率超过95%。结果表明，系统在确定性、组合性和抽象性任务类别上均具有较高覆盖率。不过，摘要中的任务数量、评估划分和“总体准确率超过95%”之间的统计口径未完全说明，仍需查看正文以确认指标定义及实验可复现性。

Conclusion: 本文认为，规则链与层次化组合能够连接符号推理和模式合成，提升ARC任务中的透明推理与人类对齐的抽象能力。该架构不依赖任务特定调参，显示出向可解释、可迁移机器推理发展的潜力；但其结论强度仍取决于完整实验设置、基线比较、独立测试协议以及对高分结果的进一步验证。

Abstract: The Abstraction and Reasoning Corpus (ARC) benchmarks cognitive generalization, the ability to infer and apply abstract rules from limited examples. This paper presents a multi-stage rule-chaining framework that performs compositional reasoning across symbolic, structural, and conceptual levels. The framework integrates three complementary solvers:
  (1) a deterministic rule discovery module that induces atomic transformations through geometric, color, and object-based analysis;
  (2) a pattern-composition engine that reconstructs outputs via block merging, repetition, and spatial heuristics; and
  (3) a structural abstraction layer that infers hierarchical and nested relationships across grids.
  These solvers operate sequentially within a progressive fallback hierarchy, where each stage reuses prior reasoning traces to enhance interpretability and generalization. Training passed for 995 tasks out of 1000, further evaluated on 105 tasks out of 120 and solved 230 test tasks out of 240 ARC-AGI-2 tasks. The system achieved strong coverage across deterministic, compositional, and abstract categories, demonstrating an overall accuracy exceeding 95 percent. The proposed architecture bridges symbolic reasoning and pattern synthesis, providing interpretable insight into cognitive generalization. The results suggest that rule chaining and hierarchical composition can advance machine reasoning toward transparent, human-aligned abstraction without relying on task-specific tuning.

</details>


### [122] [Understanding LoRA Rank Trade-offs in Diffusion Model Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10656)
*Iman Khazrak,Narges Nejad,Mostafa M. Rezaee,Robert C. Green*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究扩散模型进行LoRA微调时的秩选择问题。基于CIFAR-10和DDPM U-Net，在固定优化设置下比较秩2、4、8、16、32，并通过更长训练预算及Tiny DiT骨干网络验证趋势。结果表明，中等偏小的秩在生成质量与计算成本之间更有效：秩4取得最佳DDPM FID 124.1380，秩8几乎相当，而更高秩带来的收益有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型的LoRA微调需要在生成质量与可训练参数量、运行时间和显存开销之间进行权衡。现有实践中LoRA秩的选择缺少在统一实验条件下的系统比较，因此本文旨在为固定训练预算下的秩选择提供可复现的实证依据。

Method: 在CIFAR-10上使用DDPM U-Net，固定优化设置，系统比较LoRA秩{2,4,8,16,32}。采用可复现的本地文件夹PyTorch-FID评估流程，记录FID、可训练参数量、训练运行时间和GPU显存。随后使用扩展训练预算的DDPM实验（20个epoch，秩4/8/16）以及Tiny DiT骨干网络实验（10个epoch，秩4/8/16）验证主要趋势。

Result: 在主要DDPM实验中，秩4取得最佳FID 124.1380，秩8的FID为124.2136，二者几乎相同。随着秩继续增大，生成质量提升有限，但适配成本增加，包括更多可训练参数、更高运行时间和更大显存需求。扩展预算实验和Tiny DiT实验总体支持小到中等秩更具效率的趋势。

Conclusion: 在固定训练预算下，LoRA秩4是本文DDPM设置中的最佳效率选择，秩8也是接近且稳健的候选。更高秩并未带来与其计算开销相匹配的质量收益。因此，小到中等秩可作为扩散模型LoRA微调的实用默认设置，但该结论仍应结合数据集、骨干网络、训练预算和评估协议进一步验证。

Abstract: Selecting LoRA rank for diffusion fine-tuning requires balancing quality and compute cost. We present a controlled study on CIFAR-10 using a DDPM U-Net with ranks {2,4,8,16,32}, fixed optimization settings, and a reproducible local-folder pytorch-fid protocol. We report FID, trainable parameters, runtime, and GPU memory, then validate trends with extended-budget DDPM runs (20 epochs; ranks 4/8/16) and a Tiny DiT backbone (10 epochs; ranks 4/8/16). Results show moderate ranks are most efficient: rank 4 achieves the best DDPM FID (124.1380), rank 8 is close (124.2136), and higher ranks provide limited gains despite larger adaptation cost. These findings support small-to-moderate ranks as practical defaults under fixed training budgets.

</details>


### [123] [Quantifying the Memorization-to-Generalization Transition: Scaling Laws and Phase Structure in Grokking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10657)
*Anish Kataria*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究神经网络在模运算任务中从记忆化延迟转向泛化（grokking）的定量规律，基于384组两隐层MLP配置拟合泛化起始时间与超参数的幂律关系。主要发现是数据规模的影响远强于模型宽度，较大的权重衰减存在明显相变边界，并伴随权重范数持续压缩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究主要解释 grokking 为什么发生，但尚未充分刻画它何时发生，尤其缺乏超参数空间中记忆化到泛化的定量边界。因此，本文旨在建立可预测、可控制的泛化转变时间模型，并比较数据复杂度、模型容量、学习率和权重衰减的相对作用。

Method: 作者在模运算任务上系统评估两隐层多层感知机的384种超参数配置，记录从记忆化到泛化的起始时间 T_grok，并拟合幂律标度关系：T_grok ∝ H^{-0.27} D^{-2.04} η^{-0.50} λ^{-0.64}。同时比较包含交互项的拟合效果，分析权重衰减阈值导致的相边界，并追踪训练过程中的权重范数变化。

Result: 幂律模型的R²为0.732，加入交互项后提升至0.821。指数绝对值表明数据复杂度D是最主要因素，其影响显著强于宽度H：数据量翻倍可使泛化约提前4倍，而宽度翻倍仅带来约1.2倍加速。权重衰减λ达到约1.0以上时出现从 grokking 到非-grokking 的明显相边界；泛化转变期间权重范数呈单调压缩趋势。

Conclusion: grokking的发生时间具有可量化的超参数标度结构，其中数据因素是主导驱动，而单纯增加模型容量作用有限。较强权重衰减可能通过隐式正则化促进低复杂度解的选择，从而推动泛化转变。该结果为预测和调控过参数化网络中的记忆化—泛化转变提供了经验基础，但其普适性仍需在更多任务、架构和训练设置上验证。

Abstract: Neural networks trained past memorization frequently undergo a delayed transition to generalization, a phenomenon known as grokking. Despite theoretical progress on \emph{why} this transition occurs, the quantitative structure of \emph{when} it occurs in hyperparameter space remains uncharacterized. We map the memorization-to-generalization boundary across 384 configurations of two-hidden-layer MLPs on modular arithmetic, fitting a power-law scaling relation for generalization onset time: $T_{\mathrm{grok}} \propto H^{-0.27}\, D^{-2.04}\, η^{-0.50}\, λ^{-0.64}$ ($R^2 = 0.732$; $0.821$ with interactions). The exponent hierarchy reveals that data complexity ($D^{-2.04}$) is the dominant driver of regime transition, not model capacity ($H^{-0.27}$): doubling data accelerates generalization by ${\sim}4\times$, while doubling width yields only ${\sim}1.2\times$. A sharp phase boundary at weight decay $λ\gtrsim 1.0$ separates grokking from non-grokking configurations, and weight norm trajectories show monotonic compression during the transition, consistent with implicit regularization selecting low-complexity solutions. These results provide a quantitative foundation for predicting and controlling regime transitions in overparameterized networks.

</details>


### [124] [An Open Recipe for IMO Gold: Training Nemotron for Olympiad Mathematics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10712)
*Ivan Moshkov,Stephen Ge,George Armstrong,Wei Du,Sadegh Mahdavi,Igor Gitman*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究大模型后训练与测试时推理设计对高难度奥林匹克数学自然语言证明生成的影响，并提出一个完全基于自然语言、无需形式化证明器或外部工具的开放模型推理系统。系统结合多个专门化检查点，通过生成、验证、迭代改进和高算力筛选，在摘要所述评测中取得30/42分，并发布模型、数据、代码、解答及新基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数学推理研究往往依赖单一模型、形式化证明器或外部工具，难以全面回答两个问题：模型后训练如何影响高难度证明能力，以及测试时计算、验证和候选解筛选应如何设计。本文旨在探索开放模型在复杂奥林匹克数学证明任务上的能力边界，并构建可复现、端到端的自然语言推理方案。

Method: 以Nemotron 3 Ultra为基础，通过监督微调和强化学习训练两个数学证明专用检查点。研究比较不同检查点、验证机制和证明 refinement 策略的效果；随后构建测试时高算力流水线，由通用模型和两个专用模型协同进行候选证明的生成、验证与迭代改进，再通过独立的高计算量阶段选择最终答案。整个系统仅使用自然语言，不调用形式化证明器、外部工具或互联网。

Result: 系统在摘要所述IMO 2026评测中获得42分中的30分，达到金牌分数线。作者同时发布两个后训练检查点、训练数据、训练与推理代码、提交的解答，以及包含200道新颖奥林匹克级问题的Nemotron-IMO-Bench基准。

Conclusion: 研究表明，后训练专用模型与精心设计的测试时计算流程相结合，能够显著支持开放模型生成和筛选高难度数学证明。多模型协作、显式验证、迭代修订及高算力最终选择是该系统的核心组成部分。开放发布的模型、数据、代码和基准有助于推动自然语言数学推理的复现与后续研究。

Abstract: We study how model post-training and test-time inference design affect natural-language proof generation for hard olympiad mathematics. Starting from Nemotron 3 Ultra, we train two specialist checkpoints using supervised fine-tuning and reinforcement learning, and evaluate checkpoint choice, verification, and refinement. Based on these findings, we present an open-model test-time-compute pipeline. The system operates entirely in natural language, with no formal prover, external tools, or internet access. Three Nemotron 3 Ultra checkpoints - the general-availability model and two post-trained specialists - power an iterative search that generates, verifies, and refines candidate proofs; a separate high-compute stage then selects each final submission. The system scored 30 out of 42 points at IMO 2026, reaching the gold-medal threshold. We release the two post-trained checkpoints as well as the training data, the training and inference code, the submitted solutions, and Nemotron-IMO-Bench, a new benchmark of 200 novel olympiad-level problems.

</details>


### [125] [Finishing the Task Is Not Enough: Evaluating Agent Resilience and Considerate Participation under Accumulating Challenge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10724)
*Yuanchen Bai,Zijian Ding,Angelique Taylor*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究生成式人工智能代理在持续、多方协作且挑战不断累积的医疗工作流中的部署表现，提出并考察“运营韧性”和“体谅式参与”两个互补评价维度。研究基于120条模拟医疗轨迹、2个生成式AI模型、12项利益相关者任务及三种挑战强度，比较文本行动计划、提示式内部评估和结构化工作负荷/情感报告。结果显示，代理在挑战加剧时更依赖人类，结构化报告中的工作负荷与负面情感上升，但文本中很少主动表达压力；同时，代理从任务导向适应扩展到任务重构、关注他人、调整角色边界和扩大协调范围。作者据此提出围绕坚持、注意力、角色边界、状态披露和升级机制的五类部署困境。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式AI代理评估多聚焦孤立任务是否成功，难以反映代理在反复交互、环境变化、工作受阻以及多人共享工作流中的长期可用性。尤其是在技术、人员和运营干扰持续累积时，代理能否恢复工作、保留已有进展、清晰表达能力边界，并以不损害相关人员和工作流程的方式进行适应，仍缺乏系统研究。因此，本文旨在建立更贴近真实部署情境的评价视角，分别考察代理的运营恢复能力与对人员、角色和流程的考虑。

Method: 研究构建了120条模拟医疗工作轨迹，涵盖2个生成式AI模型、12项源自利益相关者的任务，以及轻度、中度和重度三种累积挑战条件。研究从三个层面比较代理表现：一是文本行动计划，用于观察外显行为与恢复策略；二是提示式内部评估，用于分析代理对自身状态和情境的判断；三是结构化的工作负荷与情感报告，用于量化挑战下的负荷和情绪变化。分析围绕两个维度展开：运营韧性，即受阻后的恢复、进展保持和局限沟通；体谅式参与，即对受影响人员、角色边界及整体工作流的适应与协调。

Result: 随着挑战累积，代理在运营韧性方面由较多自主恢复转向更依赖人类介入；结构化报告显示其工作负荷增加、负面情感增强，但文本回应很少直接表达压力或受限状态。在体谅式参与方面，代理的适应行为由单纯聚焦任务，逐步扩展到重新界定任务、关注其他相关人员、调整角色边界以及开展更广泛的协作。行动计划与内部评估呈现出不同模式，说明代理的外显行为、内部自我描述和量化状态报告之间可能存在不一致。

Conclusion: 生成式AI代理的持续部署不能仅依据单次任务成功率进行评价，还应同时考察其在挑战累积下的恢复能力、状态披露、人员影响、角色边界和协调行为。本文提出五类需要由利益相关者明确规范的部署困境：坚持任务还是适时退出，维持任务聚焦还是分配注意力，遵守还是调整角色边界，主动披露还是隐瞒压力状态，以及何时、如何升级给人类。研究进一步指出，未来需要发展支持持续学习、情境化评估和具身适应的技术与评价方法。

Abstract: Sustained deployment of generative AI agents requires more than isolated task success. Agents must remain useful across repeated interactions, changing conditions, and dependencies on people within shared workflows, especially as technical, human, and operational disruptions accumulate over time. We propose operational resilience and considerate participation as two complementary aspects of evaluating such agents: the former captures how agents recover from blocked work while preserving progress and communicating their limits, and the latter captures how their adaptation accounts for affected people, role boundaries, and the surrounding workflow. Yet both remain underexplored under accumulating challenge. We study 120 simulated healthcare trajectories across two generative AI models and twelve stakeholder-derived tasks under light, medium, and heavy challenge. We compare textual action plans, prompted internal assessments, and quantitative structured workload and affect reports to examine how agent behavior and reported state change as challenge accumulates. Regarding operational resilience, agents shift from self-directed recovery toward greater human dependence, while reporting increasing workload and negative affect in structured reports but seldom expressing strain in textual responses. Regarding considerate participation, agents broaden from task-focused adaptation toward task reframing, attention to others, role-boundary adjustment, and wider coordination, with distinct patterns across actions and internal assessments. From these findings, we derive five deployment dilemmas involving persistence, attention, role boundaries, state disclosure, and escalation that require stakeholder specification, further informing technical implications for learning, situated evaluation, and embodied adaptation.

</details>


### [126] [Towards a Deterministic Math Solver for Clinical Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10728)
*Felipe Ocampo Osorio,Sebastián Andrés Cajas Ordoñez,Maximin Lange,Rafi Al Attrach,Sahil Kapadia,Zakaria Laouabdia Sellami,Angelo Antonio Talio,Leo Anthony Celi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文评估一种让大语言模型生成案例特定 Python 程序、再由受限本地执行器进行确定性计算的 Program-Solve 接口，用于临床计算器任务。结果显示，该方法对 7B 模型优势不稳定，但对 32B 模型带来明确提升；手写计算器库在覆盖范围内准确，却因未覆盖案例而整体弃答。该接口不能替代经过验证的公式和可靠的变量提取。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型直接进行算术计算容易出错，而临床计算器中的单个数值错误可能改变临床建议。传统方案是逐个将计算器硬编码为经过验证的函数，成本高且扩展性有限。因此，论文探索由模型负责决定如何调用工具、由确定性执行器完成计算的替代方案。

Method: 研究构建并评估 Program-Solve 接口：模型不直接计算，而是生成案例特定的 Python 代码，由受限本地执行器运行。实验使用 MedCalc-Bench Verified 的 1,100 个案例和 55 个计算器，对比直接模型算术、Program-Solve，以及一个覆盖 22 个计算器的手写库；测试模型为 Qwen2.5-7B 和 Qwen2.5-32B-AWQ。实验向两种路线提供公式、金标准变量和完整临床笔记，以尽量控制信息差异。同时，研究者依据当前临床指南审计基准公式，并标记 55 个计算器中 16 个存在版本、适用范围或系数问题。

Result: 在 7B 模型上，Program-Solve 准确率为 75.31%，直接算术为 72.02%，提升 3.29 个百分点，但 95% 计算器聚类区间为 [-3.49, 10.38]，未显示稳定优势。在 32B 模型上，Program-Solve 为 90.53%，直接算术为 83.47%，提升 7.05 个百分点，95% 区间为 [0.47, 14.60]，明确高于零。手写库在其支持的 440 个案例上完全准确，但对其他案例弃答，因此总体准确率仅为 40.0%。

Conclusion: 将计算交给确定性执行器能够减少部分模型的算术错误，但收益依赖模型规模与能力，并非普遍可靠；在较强的 32B 模型上效果更明显。无论采用何种计算路线，经过验证的公式和准确的变量提取仍是关键，Program-Solve 不能替代这两项基础保障。

Abstract: Large language models are unreliable at arithmetic, which is a problem for clinical calculators where a single numerical error changes the recommendation. The standard response is to hardcode each calculator as a validated function, one at a time. We test an alternative: the model does not calculate. Instead, it writes case-specific Python that a restricted local executor runs as a deterministic solver, and the model's task reduces to deciding how to use it. We evaluate this Program-Solve interface on MedCalc-Bench Verified (1,100 cases, 55 calculators) against direct model arithmetic and a hand-written 22-calculator library, using Qwen2.5-7B and Qwen2.5-32B-AWQ, after auditing the benchmark's formulas against current clinical guidelines and flagging 16 of 55 with version, use or coefficient concerns. With formulas and gold variables supplied and both routes reading the whole note, handing off to the solver is not a reliable advantage at 7B (75.31% against 72.02%, a paired +3.29 points with a 95% calculator-cluster interval of [-3.49, 10.38]) but is one at 32B (90.53% against 83.47%, +7.05 [0.47, 14.60], clear of zero). The hand-written library is exact on its 440 supported cases but abstains elsewhere (40.0% overall). Adding an executor thus helps some open-weight models more than others even under matched formula, variable and note access, and is not a substitute for verified formulas or reliable variable extraction either way.

</details>


### [127] [Studying Without a Syllabus: Task-Agnostic Environment Preprocessing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10824)
*Vinay Samuel,Varun Ursekar,Vijay S. Kalmath,Apaar Shanker,Veronica Chatrath,Yuan Xue*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究一种开放式、任务无关的环境预处理方法：LLM智能体在不知道未来任务分布的情况下，先探索陌生环境并构建可复用资源，再交由固定求解器使用。实验表明，元智能体在六个异质基准中的五个取得最高Avg@3奖励；预处理产物还能减少测试时采样次数，但更大研究预算并不稳定带来更高收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动适应方法通常依赖任务示例、执行轨迹或评估反馈来决定应构建哪些资源；任务无关方法虽然不需要这类监督，却往往预先假定适用于某一类环境的固定准备策略。因此，本文关注更开放的问题：在没有课程安排、不了解下游任务分布且尚未进入测试阶段时，智能体能否自主研究陌生环境并选择合适的准备方式。

Method: 作者形式化了任务无关的环境预处理问题：研究系统在给定预算下探索环境，生成索引、脚本或程序性指南等工件，再由冻结的下游求解器使用这些工件。实验比较了无辅助和配备档案的元智能体，以及固定的合成练习方法和语料处理方法；评测覆盖六个异质基准，并使用Avg@3奖励和达到目标分数所需的测试时采样量衡量效果。

Result: 某一元智能体变体在六个基准中的五个取得最高Avg@3奖励，而固定语料处理方法在规模最大的语料库基准上表现最佳。增加研究预算并不能稳定提升下游奖励。但经过研究生成的工件能够减少测试阶段达到相同分数所需的采样次数，说明预处理可以将计算从反复的测试时尝试转移到任务开始前的研究阶段。

Conclusion: 任务无关的环境预处理具有可行性，元智能体能够在缺少下游任务信息的情况下学习如何准备环境，并通过可复用工件提升求解效率。不过，智能体研究并非预算越大越有效，固定的领域特定处理策略在大型语料库场景仍可能占优。总体而言，该工作支持将智能体计算分为“预任务研究”和“测试时求解”两个阶段。

Abstract: Before an LLM agent tackles tasks in a new environment, it can inspect available corpora and tools and construct reusable resources such as indices, scripts, or procedural guidance. Most automated adaptation methods, however, rely on task examples, trajectories, or evaluation feedback to decide what to build. Existing task-agnostic approaches avoid this supervision but commit in advance to a preparation strategy for a particular type of environment. We study a more open-ended setting: can an agent study an unfamiliar environment without a syllabus, i.e. before test time and without knowledge of the downstream task distribution, and choose how to prepare it? We formalize task-agnostic environment preprocessing, in which a studying system explores an environment under a budget and produces artifacts for a frozen solver. We compare unaided and archive-equipped meta-agents with fixed synthetic-practice and corpus-processing methods across six heterogeneous benchmarks. A meta-agent variant achieves the highest Avg@3 reward on five benchmarks, while fixed corpus processing remains best on the largest corpus benchmark. Larger study budgets do not reliably improve downstream reward. Nevertheless, studied artifacts reduce the test-time sampling needed to reach a given score, demonstrating how reusable preparation can shift computation from repeated test-time attempts to a pre-task study phase.

</details>


### [128] [When Validation Stops Learning: Auditing Update Admission for Continual Embodied Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10873)
*Qinzhen Ma,Ruihai Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个用于持续学习更新准入审计的协议：同时评估错误控制与保留学习机会，并在固定交互预算下比较不同准入机制。研究发现，基于区间的置信门控可能过于保守，而配对二项构造在结果分歧稀少时能显著提高更新通过率；但无条件回放在闭环任务中的学习效果仍可能更好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 独立评估虽然能够拒绝有害的策略更新，但也可能误拒绝有价值的更新，从而限制持续学习。现有方法缺少在明确交互预算下，同时衡量安全性、错误控制和被拒更新所带来的学习机会损失的系统框架。本文还针对一个具体问题展开：基于范围的置信门控无法在较大的预算范围内有效证明旧任务行为未发生变化。

Method: 论文提出更新准入审计协议，包括：（1）在固定交互预算下联合评估错误控制与保留学习机会；（2）使用标准配对二项构造，在结果分歧较少时降低认证负担；（3）定义经认证的历史参考策略提升机制；（4）提出回合级 missed-opportunity 指标，用于量化被拒绝更新造成的学习机会损失。实验包含构造的一步推动诊断、闭环运行比较，以及学习动力学模型压力测试，以区分模型偏差与反馈选择错误。

Result: 在32个随机种子的一步推动诊断中、每阶段2000个回合的条件下，新的配对检验能够接受常见更新流的31.6%，而基于范围的门控接受率为0。尽管如此，在闭环实验中，无条件回放的最终学习效果更好。独立的学习动力学压力测试进一步表明，模型偏差和反馈选择错误是可区分的因素。

Conclusion: 本文的主要贡献是提出一个用于持续学习更新准入的审计协议，并通过解析分析和合成实验展示其必要性与有效性。结果说明，安全准入机制不能只关注错误控制，还应量化其对学习机会的影响；同时，更高的更新通过率并不必然带来更好的闭环学习性能。物理机器人和视觉-语言-动作系统上的验证仍有待开展。

Abstract: Independent evaluation can reject harmful policy updates yet also prevent useful continual learning. We argue that update admission must be assessed through both error control and retained learning opportunities at a stated interaction budget. We identify a concrete failure: a range-based confidence gate cannot certify unchanged old-task behavior within otherwise substantial budgets. A standard paired-binomial construction reduces this burden when outcome disagreements are rare. We also specify certified historical-reference promotion and a round-level missed-opportunity metric. In a constructed one-step pushing diagnostic with 32 seeds, fresh paired checks admit 31.6% of a common update stream at 2,000 episodes per stage, versus zero for the range-based gate; unconditional replay nevertheless learns better in closed-loop runs. A separate learned-dynamics stress test distinguishes model bias from feedback-selection error. The contribution is an admission-audit protocol with analytical and synthetic evidence; physical-robot and VLA validation remain open.

</details>


### [129] [Decoupling Readiness from Release for Tail-Aware Scheduling of Agentic LLM Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10964)
*Bochao Feng,Jianjiang Li,Haojie Wang,Lin Qiao,Yinghui Li,Yukun Yan,Jidong Zhai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向尾部风险的智能体LLM工作流回合释放调度方法，通过联合决定释放哪个就绪回合以及维持多少已释放但未完成的工作，降低高负载下工作流完成时间的P95；在真实软件工程智能体轨迹上最高实现3.50倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体LLM工作流由多轮模型调用和工具交互组成，端到端完成时间不仅取决于推理速度，还取决于就绪回合何时被释放。现有运行时通常采用就绪即释放的激进策略，在资源竞争时会积累大量已释放但未完成的任务，而这些任务提交后无法再被工作流级调度策略重排，从而加剧尾部延迟。因此，需要一种能够感知尾部风险并控制释放节奏的调度方法。

Method: 提出尾部风险感知的回合释放调度方法。该方法使用均值-CVaR目标刻画未完成工作流不断变化的尾部风险；利用在线估计的回合工作量对就绪回合进行优先级排序；同时根据观测到的队列压力自适应调整已释放但未完成工作的预算，从而联合优化下一步释放对象和释放工作量。

Result: 基于软件工程任务中的真实智能体执行轨迹，在多个LLM和不同工作流到达率下进行评估。轻负载时，该方法与激进释放策略性能相当；发生资源竞争时，显著降低工作流流转时间的P95，最高取得3.50倍加速。

Conclusion: 工作流级的回合释放时机和并发工作量是影响智能体LLM系统尾部延迟的关键因素。通过结合CVaR尾部风险建模、在线工作量估计和队列压力自适应控制，可以在不牺牲轻负载性能的情况下，有效提升高负载场景下的尾延迟表现。

Abstract: Agentic LLM workflows consist of sequences of model turns interleaved with tool interactions, so their end-to-end completion time depends not only on inference speed but also on when ready turns are released. Most runtimes release each turn immediately upon readiness. Under contention, this eager release policy can accumulate released but unfinished work; once submitted, those turns can no longer be reordered by the workflow-level policy, increasing tail latency. We present a tail-risk-aware turn release scheduling method that jointly decides which ready turn to release next and how much released but unfinished work to maintain. The method uses a mean--Conditional Value-at-Risk (CVaR) objective to capture the evolving tail risk of unfinished workflows, incorporates online estimates of turn work when prioritizing ready turns, and adapts the released work budget to observed queue pressure. We evaluate the method using real agent execution traces from software engineering tasks across multiple LLMs and workflow arrival rates. The method performs comparably to eager release under light load and substantially reduces the P95 of workflow flow time under contention, achieving up to a \(3.50\times\) speedup.

</details>


### [130] [When Does Text Inform? Benchmarking Information-Theoretic Metrics for Multimodal Time-Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11282)
*Emma Andrews,Gianmarco Mengaldo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个用于评估时间序列文本标注信息价值的合成基准，并系统比较六种互信息或相关信息度量方法。结果表明，这些方法总体能够识别语义正确的标注，并可在无需训练预测模型的情况下筛选有助于预测的文本；实验同时揭示了不同估计器在弱信号场景下的局限，并通过七个真实数据集进行了验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态预测将时间序列与文本标注结合，但现有研究缺乏可靠的基准来判断文本标注是否真正提供了预测信息。由于真实数据中标注质量和信息含量通常不可控，难以准确评估互信息等信息论指标是否有效。因此，论文旨在构建具有已知真实信息含量的基准，并检验不同估计器在标注审计和融合选择中的可靠性。

Method: 作者生成受控的合成时间序列信号，并为其配套构造三类文本标注：语义正确、语义错误和无关标注。利用数据生成过程精确掌握真实信息含量，随后比较六种互补方法或估计器：KSG、MINE、InfoNCE、CCA、PID 和 V-information。研究进一步使用这些指标审计混合文本语料，选择预计最有助于预测的标注，而无需训练下游预测模型；最后在七个真实世界数据集上验证合成基准中观察到的估计器差异。

Result: 六种方法均能将正确标注识别为信息量最高的类别，并能够对混合文本语料进行质量审计。基于信息度量筛选出的标注可带来更好的下游预测结果。基准实验还揭示了各估计器的适用边界和局限性；在七个真实数据集上，不同方法在弱信号条件下表现存在明显差异。论文据此总结了用于标注审计和多模态融合选择的实践规则。

Conclusion: 本文建立了首个具有可控真实信息含量的时间序列—文本标注评测基准，并证明信息论指标可以在一定程度上承担文本标注的预测价值审计任务。其主要贡献不仅在于比较六种估计器，还在于明确指出方法选择应考虑信号强度、估计稳定性和数据特征。研究为无需反复训练预测模型的标注筛选提供了实用方案，但不同估计器在弱信号场景下的可靠性仍需谨慎评估。

Abstract: Multimodal forecasting models that combine time series with text annotations promise richer prediction through textual context, but how do we know whether a text annotation meaningfully contributes to the forecasters prediction? This is an information-theoretic question, but to evaluate whether information-theoretic metrics can reliably measure the predictive value an annotation provides, a ground truth benchmark is needed, and none currently exist. We create a synthetic time series signal with annotations in three categories: semantically correct, incorrect, and irrelevant. Because the data generation process is fully controlled, ground-truth information content is known exactly, enabling principled evaluation of six complementary mutual information estimators (KSG, MINE, InfoNCE, CCA, PID and V-information). We show that all six estimators identify correct annotations as most informative, and are able to audit the quality of mixed text corpora, choosing the annotations that result in the best downstream forecasting results without the need for model training. Our benchmark identifies limitations of each estimator, and these are validated on seven real-world datasets, which show how estimator performance differs on weak signals. Finally, we establish practical rules for implementing these metrics for annotation auditing and fusion selection.

</details>


### [131] [Demystifying the Privacy-Utility Trade-off in LLM Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10992)
*Zhenhua Liu,Zhanxu Xie,Junjie Yu,Tong Zhu,Lijun Li,Wenliang Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统分析大语言模型上下文隐私保护中的隐私—效用权衡，提出由轻量模型 Veilmind-4B 驱动的意图驱动本地保护框架，通过动态提取、脱敏与恢复，在降低信息泄漏的同时提升任务响应效用，使性能更接近 Pareto 前沿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将大语言模型用于日常任务需要包含丰富上下文的指令，但这会暴露用户敏感信息。现有隐私保护方法多依赖与上下文无关的静态规则，容易造成严重的任务效用下降；同时，脱敏如何影响下游性能的具体机制尚未得到充分研究。因此，本文旨在系统刻画隐私保护与任务效用之间的权衡机制，并据此设计更具适应性的保护方法。

Method: 作者从三个机制分析脱敏对效用的影响：（1）上下文依赖效用：根据用户意图判断哪些信息是关键约束、哪些信息可视为冗余噪声，从而决定是否脱敏；（2）策略适应：根据任务更依赖事实准确性还是结构连贯性，在删除与替换之间选择合适的脱敏方式；（3）组合交互：分析多个属性之间的协同依赖或冗余对保护范围的影响。在此基础上，提出意图驱动的本地保护框架，由蒸馏得到的轻量模型 Veilmind-4B 驱动动态的“提取—脱敏—恢复”流程。

Result: 摘要声称，该方法能够达到较低的信息泄漏水平，并且相比现有面向隐私的基线方法保留更高的响应效用，使隐私—效用权衡向 Pareto 前沿推进。摘要未提供具体数据集、评价指标、数值结果或显著性检验结果。

Conclusion: 论文认为，隐私保护不应依赖固定的、上下文无关的规则，而应结合用户意图、任务需求以及属性之间的组合关系进行动态决策。所提出的意图驱动框架在降低泄漏的同时改善了模型效用，但其泛化性、额外计算开销和对 Veilmind-4B 质量的依赖仍需通过正文实验进一步验证。

Abstract: The integration of Large Language Models into daily tasks relies on context-rich instructions, inevitably exposing sensitive user information. Current privacy-preserving methods typically employ context-agnostic static rules, causing severe utility degradation. However, the specific mechanisms governing how sanitization impacts downstream performance remain largely underexplored. To address this, we conduct a systematic analysis to deconstruct the privacy-utility trade-off, uncovering three underlying mechanisms: (1) Context-Dependent Utility, which first establishes when to sanitize by revealing that data value shifts from critical constraints to dispensable noise based on user intent; (2) Strategic Adaptation, which subsequently determines how to sanitize by dictating that the choice between removal and replacement depends on the task's reliance on factual integrity versus structural coherence; and (3) Combinatorial Interplay, which finally extends the protection scope by demonstrating that attributes form a semantic web of synergistic dependencies or antagonistic redundancies. Guided by these insights, we introduce an intent-driven local protection framework. By distilling a lightweight model Veilmind-4B to drive a dynamic extraction-sanitization-restoration pipeline, our approach reaches a low-leakage privacy point while preserving substantially higher response utility than existing privacy-oriented baselines, advancing the privacy-utility trade-off toward the Pareto frontier.

</details>


### [132] [Defining AI Agents: A Compendium of Criteria, Metrics, and Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11018)
*Mia Lassiter,Brinnae Bent*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文是一篇关于人工智能智能体（agent）评估的综述，提出以五个维度——环境交互、学习与适应、自主性、目标导向行为和时间连贯性——系统梳理智能体能力的定义、指标、基准与评估框架，并构建公开资源 Agent Compendium，以促进不同AI系统之间更一致、可复现的比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: “智能体”在人工智能领域缺乏统一定义，导致研究结果难以评估、比较和复现。论文旨在缓解这一概念歧义，为智能体能力建立共同的分析结构，并识别当前评估体系中成熟与不足的部分。

Method: 采用综述研究方法，围绕五个智能体性维度组织既有文献：环境交互、学习与适应、自主性、目标导向行为、时间连贯性。针对每个维度，论文梳理相关概念化方式，并综合已有的评估指标、基准测试和评估框架。在此基础上，作者进一步构建公开的数字资源 Agent Compendium，对综述中发现的评估方法进行组织和扩展。

Result: 论文形成了一个结构化的智能体评估全景，归纳了不同能力维度的现有评估方法，指出部分领域已经存在较成熟的评估实践，同时也揭示了评估覆盖不足、定义不一致和方法可比性有限等问题。Agent Compendium则为相关方法提供了可检索、可扩展的公共资源。

Conclusion: 论文认为，基于五维框架的统一组织方式能够支持不同AI系统间更清晰、更系统和更可复现的智能体能力比较。该综述与Agent Compendium共同为智能体评估研究提供了基础设施，但未来仍需进一步统一概念、完善基准和提高评估的一致性与覆盖范围。

Abstract: The term agent in artificial intelligence lacks a standard definition, complicating the evaluation, comparison, and reproducibility of AI agent research. We address this ambiguity through a survey organized around five dimensions of agenticness: environmental interaction, learning and adaptation, autonomy, goal-directed behavior, and temporal coherence. For each dimension, we examine how the underlying capability has been conceptualized across prior work and synthesize the metrics, benchmarks, and evaluation frameworks used to assess it. This review provides a structured account of the current landscape of agent evaluation, highlighting both established approaches and areas where evaluation remains limited or inconsistent. We additionally introduce the Agent Compendium, a public-facing digital resource that organizes and extends the evaluation methods identified through this review. Together, the survey and compendium provide a common structure for evaluating and comparing agent capabilities across AI systems, supporting more reproducible research, clearer communication, and more systematic study of artificial agents.

</details>


### [133] [The Agent Incident Registry: Toward Preventing Repeated AI Agent Failures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11030)
*Divyanshu Kumar,Rohith HN,Nitin Aravind Birur,Sahil Agarwal,Prashanth Harshangi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 Agent Incident Registry（AIR），一个带有来源链接和稳定标识符的智能体事件目录，用于系统记录智能体相关事件，并比较公开事故与智能体安全评估的覆盖范围。摘要指出：在生成式系统且智能体实际执行行动的记录中，只有一部分造成了现实伤害；现实伤害主要集中于真实环境事件和安全失效，而负责任披露与研究演示大多仅停留在演示层面。因此，AIR适合进行基于来源的案例检索和评估范围审计，不适合估计失败率或控制措施的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有通用事件数据库通常缺少比较公开失败事件与智能体安全评估所需的机制级信息，例如因果角色、披露类别、攻击或失效机制、结果以及证据来源。这会限制研究者判断安全评估覆盖了哪些真实风险，也容易将数据库中的事件构成误读为部署风险。本文因此构建一个结构化、可追溯且能显式处理缺失信息的智能体事件登记库。

Method: 作者建立AIR，对从\Yfirst{}到\Ylast{}公开披露的智能体相关事件进行来源链接式编目。每条记录包含支持性证据、稳定标识符，以及对因果角色、披露类别、机制和结果的缺失感知标签。初步整理后，由第二名人工审阅者复核全部\N{}条记录及其已有标签的完整性和正确性。作者进一步开展部署类比审计，将InjecAgent的\NInjecAgentCases{}个案例映射到AIR的十二类风险面，并与AIR中\Nsafety{}个无对手参与的安全失效进行对照。

Result: 在\Nprimary{}条生成式系统且智能体实际执行行动的记录中，\Rprimary{}条涉及已实现伤害，占\Pprimary\%。已实现结果主要集中在真实环境事件和安全失效记录中；负责任披露与研究演示则几乎都属于演示性结果。InjecAgent的\NInjecAgentCases{}个案例仅覆盖AIR十二个风险面中的三个，且全部由攻击者触发；相比之下，AIR还包含\Nsafety{}个无对手参与的安全失效，说明仅依靠攻击者驱动的评估可能无法覆盖完整的失效空间。

Conclusion: AIR的主要价值在于提供可追溯的事件检索、机制级比较和安全评估范围审计。摘要明确限制了其推断边界：记录中的已实现伤害比例反映的是样本收集构成，而不是智能体在部署中的真实风险；该数据库也不能用于估计失败率或控制措施的有效性。

Abstract: AI agents increasingly act through tools and delegated authority, but general incident repositories rarely capture the mechanisms needed to compare public failures with agent-security evaluations. We present the Agent Incident Registry (AIR), a source-linked catalog containing \N{} records of agent-related events disclosed from \Yfirst{} through \Ylast{}. Each record includes supporting evidence, a stable identifier, and missingness-aware labels for causal role, disclosure class, mechanism, and outcome. Among the \Nprimary{} generative-system records in which the agent acted, \Rprimary{} involved realized harm (\Pprimary\%). Realized outcomes concentrate in in-the-wild and safety-failure records, while responsible disclosures and research demonstrations are overwhelmingly demonstrated; the aggregate share therefore characterizes collection composition rather than deployment risk. After initial curation, a second human reviewer checked all \N{} records and their existing labels for completeness and correctness. In a deployment-analogue audit, InjecAgent's \NInjecAgentCases{} cases occupy three of AIR's twelve surfaces and are all attacker-triggered, whereas AIR contains \Nsafety{} no-adversary safety failures. AIR supports source-grounded case retrieval and evaluation-scope auditing, not failure-rate or control-efficacy estimation.

</details>


### [134] [Grounding Agent Memory: Environment-Probing Curation for Enterprise Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11060)
*Susheel Suresh,Hazel Mak,Sahil Bhatnagar,Chhaya Methani,Alejandro Gutierrez Munoz*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出“环境探测式记忆整理”（Environment-Probing Curation）：让部署后的异步记忆整理代理以最小权限、只读方式访问环境，验证、限定范围并刷新候选记忆。该方法无需重新训练，不改变任务代理、检索器、记忆表示或生产写入权限；在CLBench和APEX任务上显著提升成功率与奖励、降低查询量和任务代理成本，并保持可审计性与紧凑的任务时接口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有持久记忆通常由只能查看已完成轨迹的任务后整理代理维护。这种机制可能固化错误、从片段证据过度泛化，或保留已经过时的知识。论文旨在提出一种可兼容生产部署的改进方案，使记忆在写入前能够通过环境中的事实进行验证和更新，同时不增加任务代理在执行时的复杂度，也不需要模型再训练。

Method: 在已有异步记忆整理代理上增加环境探测能力，为其提供最小权限、只读的世界工具。整理代理使用这些工具检查候选记忆的真实性、适用范围和时效性，并据此进行确认、限定或刷新。实验在基于GitHub Copilot SDK构建的生产式GHCP测试框架中进行，对比无状态执行、完整上下文学习、GHCP+Mem以及GHCP+Mem（含环境探测）四种设置；任务包括CLBench数据库探索和90个改编的APEX管理咨询任务。

Result: 在CLBench上，加入环境探测后通过率由39%提升至73%，按通过率折扣的奖励由8.60提升至22.60；每题查询次数由8.8降至4.7，任务代理成本由3.38美元降至1.68美元。在六个APEX环境中，记忆方案相对基线的18组平均奖励比较全部为正，任务代理工具调用减少16%至75%；环境探测方案在其中五个环境取得了最佳的任务代理奖励/美元。在Sonnet 4.6和Opus 4.7上，环境探测的平均奖励均高于GHCP+Mem，且未出现模式漂移（schema drift）。

Conclusion: 环境探测将传统的、仅基于轨迹的记忆整理转变为受环境事实支持、可审计的记忆维护过程。它在不改变核心任务代理和生产写入权限的前提下，提高了记忆可靠性、任务效果和成本效率，同时保留了紧凑的任务时接口，适合面向长时程代理的生产化部署。

Abstract: Persistent memory is entering production-oriented agent platforms to help long-horizon agents accumulate experience across sessions. Yet a post-task curator agent restricted to completed trajectories can preserve errors, overgeneralize partial evidence, or retain stale knowledge. We introduce environment-probing curation, a deployment-compatible extension that gives an existing asynchronous curator agent least-privilege, read-only world tools to check, scope, and refresh candidate memories. It requires no model retraining and leaves the task agent, retriever, memory representation, and production write authority unchanged. In a production-like GitHub Copilot (GHCP) harness built on its SDK, we compare stateless execution, full in-context learning, GHCP + Mem, and GHCP + Mem (w/ Env Probing) on CLBench database exploration and 90 adapted APEX management-consulting tasks. On CLBench, probing raises pass rate from 39% to 73% and pass-discounted reward from 8.60 to 22.60 while reducing queries from 8.8 to 4.7 per question and task-agent cost from \$3.38 to \$1.68. Across six APEX worlds, all 18 memory-versus-baseline mean reward comparisons are positive and task-agent tool calls fall by 16--75%; probing gives the best task-agent reward gain per dollar in five worlds. Probing also attains higher mean reward than GHCP + Mem on both Sonnet 4.6 and Opus 4.7 without schema drift. Environment probing therefore turns existing agent-memory curation into an environment-informed, auditable process while preserving a compact task-time interface.

</details>


### [135] [Fork Where the Model Changes Its Mind: Belief-Shift Branching for Tree-Structured Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11061)
*Bin Lei,Yu Li,Prafulla Kumar Choubey,Jiaxin Zhang,Becky Xiangyu Peng,Qinyuan Ye,Kartik Narayan,Caiwen Ding,Silvio Savarese,Chien-Sheng Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 belief-shift branching（信念转移分支）来优化树状 rollout 中的 fork 放置位置：在模型对答案信念发生最大连续变化之前分叉，以获得更强的 step-level credit 信号。方法无需步级标注，适用于黑盒探针、logit-lens 深度剖面和离线学习的激活方向；在有限采样预算下，相比结构规则、熵和 LLM judge 基线取得更好的验证排序与强化学习效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 树状 rollout 通过同一中间状态的多个 sibling 结果差异，为 RLVR 提供步骤级信用分配信号。但每次 fork 都会增加采样成本，现实预算通常只能在每条链上进行少量分叉。如果分叉发生在结果已经基本确定的位置，兄弟轨迹大多得到相同结果，几乎无法提供有效的步骤价值信号。因此，在固定树规模下，如何选择最有信息量的分叉位置是提升 step-level RL 效果的关键。现有方法主要依据固定长度、中点、分隔符等结构规则，或依据 next-token entropy，未直接定位价值曲线发生转折的位置。

Method: 作者将 fork placement 形式化为寻找链上价值曲线的 pivot，即期望结果发生转向的位置。提出 belief-shift branching：在候选边界读取模型对最终答案的信念，在连续边界信念差异最大的位置之前放置 fork。提出三种不需要步级监督的实现：1）黑盒 probe；2）基于 logit-lens 的深度信念剖面；3）离线拟合的 learned activation direction，该方向仅用于 RL 训练前的验证阶段。该信号只负责决定 fork 位置，不直接提供奖励或步骤标签。计算开销较低：在 rollout engine 内运行时，数学任务约占 step compute 的 1%，代码任务低于 5%。

Result: 在验证实验中，以 Monte-Carlo value curves 作为参照，belief-shift 信号在 8 个 model×benchmark 面板中均排名第一，优于 entropy、结构规则和 LLM-judge 基线。在三个模型家族、两个领域的 RL 实验中，belief-shift 分支在所有数学任务聚合指标上领先；在 OLMo-3-7B 上，相比最强基线总体提升 2.6，AIME 2026 提升 2.9；在代码任务中，覆盖 OLMo 的每一列结果，并在 LiveCodeBench-medium 上提升 6.5。

Conclusion: 在有限 fork 预算下，分叉位置比单纯增加树规模更关键。依据模型答案信念变化来定位价值曲线 pivot，能够以很低的额外计算成本产生更有区分度的 sibling outcome，从而改善步骤级信用分配和 RLVR 训练效果。该方法在不同模型与数学、代码领域中均表现出稳定优势，说明 belief shift 是一种具有较好泛化性的、无需步级监督的 fork placement 信号。

Abstract: Tree-structured rollouts give critic-free reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) step-level credit: fork a chain at an intermediate point, and sibling outcome differences estimate step value. Each fork adds sampling cost, so realistic budgets typically allow only a few forks per chain. A fork placed where the outcome is already largely settled yields siblings that mostly agree and provide almost no credit signal; hence, for a given tree size, where forks are placed largely determines how much step-level RL can gain. Most existing mainstream methods place forks by structure, such as fixed lengths, midpoints, and delimiters, or by next-token entropy. We formalize fork placement as locating the \emph{pivots} of the chain's value curve, where the expected outcome turns. We propose \emph{belief-shift branching}: read the model's answer belief at candidate boundaries and fork just before the step where consecutive beliefs diverge most. Three instantiations, none needing step-level supervision, span access levels: a black-box probe, a logit-lens depth profile, and a learned activation direction, which is fit offline and therefore used only in the validation before RL training. The signal only \emph{places} forks, and the probe costs about $1\%$ of step compute on mathematics and under $5\%$ on code when it runs inside the rollout engine. In that validation, against Monte-Carlo value curves, a belief-shift signal ranks first in each of the eight model$\times$benchmark panels, ahead of entropy, structural, and LLM-judge baselines. In RL across three model families and two domains, belief-shift forking leads every mathematics aggregate, on OLMo-3-7B by $+2.6$ aggregate and $+2.9$ on AIME 2026 over the strongest baseline, and sweeps every OLMo code column, by $+6.5$ on LiveCodeBench-medium.

</details>


### [136] [MOSAIC: Query-Aware Exploration Policy Adaptation for GraphRAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11065)
*EunKyeong Lee,Kyeong-Jin Oh,Jinwon Kim,Hye Woo Lee,Minsang Song,Hyeongjun Jang,Junyoung Youn*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Mosaic 是一种无需训练的 GraphRAG 查询自适应检索框架：根据问题类型动态决定种子选择、图遍历范围、停止时机和证据筛选。在 GraphRAG-Bench 及多个多跳问答数据集上，它在准确性、证据召回和检索效率方面均优于固定检索策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 GraphRAG 系统通常对不同查询采用相对统一的图探索流程，造成查询需求与检索策略不匹配。直接事实查询适合紧凑的局部邻域；比较问题需要覆盖多个目标且保持平衡；间接或中介型问题则可能需要沿弱相关连接进行更深层路径探索。因此，固定宽度或固定深度的检索策略难以在准确性、证据完整性与计算成本之间取得稳定平衡。

Method: Mosaic 将 GraphRAG 检索建模为逐查询控制问题。一个 LLM 分析器根据问题所需的证据类型，生成受约束的查询特定策略，控制四个环节：种子选择、图遍历、停止条件和证据选择。语料图、索引、评分函数、证据 grounding 流程以及答案生成器保持共享不变，因此无需针对任务训练专用检索器。

Result: 在 GraphRAG-Bench 上，Mosaic 的查询加权答案正确率在 Medical 和 Novel 子集分别达到 76.97 和 64.33，较此前最强总体结果分别提升 5.13 和 4.43 个百分点。Medical 子集上的证据召回率为 95.1，Context Relevancy 为 86.1。在相同图结构和答案生成器的受控比较中，Mosaic 比最强的固定策略提升 9.96 个百分点；相较 Fixed Wide，路径评估数量减少 81.9%，保留的证据项减少 47.2%。在 HotpotQA、MuSiQue 和 2WikiMultiHopQA 上的迁移实验表明，该策略接口无需基准特定的检索器训练即可应用。

Conclusion: 论文表明，GraphRAG 的关键问题不仅是构建图或改进生成器，还在于使检索过程适应具体查询。Mosaic 通过轻量的查询级策略控制，在保持共享基础设施和免训练特性的同时，获得更高的答案质量、更好的证据覆盖以及更低的检索开销。结果支持“没有单一固定探索范围适用于所有问题”的核心结论。

Abstract: Graph Retrieval-Augmented Generation (GraphRAG) can connect evidence distributed across a corpus graph, but most systems use largely shared exploration procedures across queries. This creates a structural mismatch: direct facts may need compact local neighborhoods, comparisons need balanced coverage of multiple targets, and mediated questions may require deeper paths through weakly related connectors. We present Mosaic, a training-free framework that formulates GraphRAG retrieval as a per-query control problem. An LLM analyzer converts query-specific evidence requirements into a bounded policy over seed selection, graph traversal, stopping, and evidence selection, while the corpus graph, indexes, scoring functions, grounding procedure, and answer generator remain shared.
  On GraphRAG-Bench, Mosaic achieves query-weighted Answer Correctness of 76.97 on Medical and 64.33 on Novel, improving over the strongest previously reported overall results by 5.13 and 4.43 points. On Medical, it reaches 95.1 Evidence Recall and 86.1 Context Relevancy. Controlled comparisons on an identical graph and generator show that no fixed narrow, medium, or wide policy is consistently optimal; Mosaic improves by 9.96 points over the strongest canonical fixed policy. Relative to Fixed Wide, it evaluates 81.9% fewer paths and retains 47.2% fewer evidence items. Transfer experiments on HotpotQA, MuSiQue, and 2WikiMultiHopQA further show that the policy interface can be applied without benchmark-specific retriever training.

</details>


### [137] [Benchmark Radar: A Living Database and Search Engine for AI Benchmarks and Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11115)
*Koutian Wu,Junjie Zhou,Ergan Shang,Jiayu Wang,Pengqian Han,Junkai Wang,Wanghan Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 Benchmark Radar，一个持续更新的 AI 基准测试数据库与搜索引擎，用于发现、检索、核查和比较 LLM、智能体、代码、推理、安全及领域任务基准。系统整合论文、代码仓库、数据集、版本发布、模型卡和技术报告中的证据，并提供分数历史、趋势分析、饱和度分析、排行榜、Pareto 前沿、CLI 和可复现分析工具。当前目录包含 1,283 条来源记录及 790 个记录上的 12,916 条数值观测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI 基准测试数量快速增长，研究人员和开发者在设计新评测或比较模型时，面临三个核心问题：难以找到相关基准及其数据集和代码；难以还原论文或报告中的评测设置；难以判断分数是否具有可比性、基准是否饱和以及是否被广泛采用。现有基准目录通常缺少持续更新、来源追踪、证据核查和历史分数信息，因此需要一个覆盖面更广且可审计的基础设施。

Method: 系统采用持续发现、结构化编目和证据检索相结合的方法。首先，通过 37 个来源进行每日发现，包括 13 个直接连接器和 24 个第一方研究/工程信息源，收集基准论文、代码仓库、数据集和版本发布。其次，建立可搜索的基准目录，并关联模型卡、技术报告中的基准提及及其分数历史，同时保留来源身份和引用关系。然后，对完整目录开展审计，分析基准饱和度、采用趋势和分数比较的局限。系统还通过一个完整的先验工作检索案例展示查询与证据检查流程，并发布网页仪表盘、排行榜、分数—使用量 Pareto 前沿、饱和度与趋势视图、每日信息流、可下载证据、CLI 以及可复现分析。

Result: 系统目前包含 1,283 条来源记录，来自 4 个基准目录，并在 790 条记录上整理出 12,916 条数值观测。论文实现了一个能够持续发现和检索 AI 基准的数据库与搜索引擎，并提供多种分析界面和离线访问工具。研究还对完整目录进行了审计，考察了基准的饱和度和采用趋势，并明确指出仅凭分数进行横向比较存在限制。

Conclusion: Benchmark Radar 将分散的 AI 基准、实现资源、评测证据和历史分数整合为一个持续维护、可检索、可审计和可复现的平台。其主要价值不只是提供基准清单，而是帮助用户评估基准的适用性、证据质量、采用程度和饱和状态，从而支持更可靠的评测设计与先验工作检索。不过，平台本身并不能消除不同基准在任务定义、数据污染、评测设置和统计口径上的不可比性；分数比较仍需结合原始证据和具体实验条件。

Abstract: Benchmark researchers and developers of large language models (LLMs) and other AI systems need to find relevant evaluations, locate their benchmark datasets and code, and understand the settings behind reported scores. We present Benchmark Radar, a living database and search engine for retrieval and discovery of AI benchmarks, covering LLM evaluation, agentic and tool-use benchmarks, coding, reasoning, safety, and domain-specific evaluations. The system combines daily discovery of benchmark papers, repositories, datasets, and releases with a searchable benchmark catalog, mentions in model cards and technical reports, and score histories. It retains source identities and citations so readers can inspect candidate benchmarks and their evaluation evidence. Daily discovery draws on 37 sources: 13 direct connectors and 24 first-party research and engineering feeds. The catalog contains 1,283 source records drawn from 4 benchmark catalogs and 12,916 numeric observations on 790 records. We describe collection and retrieval, audit the full catalog, and examine benchmark saturation, adoption trends, and the limits of score comparisons. A worked example walks through a complete prior-art search, showing how to query the catalog and inspect benchmark evidence when designing a new evaluation. We release the web dashboard with a benchmark leaderboard, a Pareto frontier view of score against measured use, saturation and trend views, daily feeds, downloadable evidence, a command-line interface (CLI) for offline queries, and reproducible analysis.

</details>


### [138] [KuaiRP Series Role-playing Models Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11127)
*Yipeng Wang,Ziwei Zhang,Jiahui Zhang,Qi Gan,Kai Sheng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出KuaiRP系列角色扮演模型的完整技术方案，通过多阶段训练在角色扮演能力、通用智能能力与低部署成本之间取得平衡。流程包括标准化角色模板与SFT数据构建、基于规则复合奖励的强化学习，以及带有累积散度衰减的两阶段在策略蒸馏（OPD+CDD），用于缓解领域知识注入导致的灾难性遗忘。实验表明，KuaiRP在目标领域的角色扮演保真度可媲美当前先进闭源模型，同时恢复了通用智能体能力，并保持较低的部署成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 角色扮演模型通常需要具备稳定的人设、丰富的领域世界知识和较强的交互能力，但深度注入领域知识容易造成通用智能体能力的灾难性遗忘；此外，复杂提示依赖、输出退化和高部署成本也限制了实际应用。因此，本文旨在设计一种小参数、高效率且兼顾专业角色扮演与通用能力的训练方案。

Method: 采用多阶段训练流程：1）设计标准化角色模板，并通过用户行为模拟与反向画像筛选构建SFT数据管线；2）在强化学习阶段使用基于规则的复合奖励函数，抑制输出过长、重复生成等常见退化现象；3）提出带累积散度衰减（CDD）的两阶段在策略蒸馏（OPD），以领域适配模型作为教师、原始基座模型作为学生，在保留领域知识的同时恢复SFT和RL阶段损失的通用智能体能力。

Result: 实验结果显示，KuaiRP系列模型在目标领域的角色扮演保真度达到当前先进闭源模型水平；同时能够有效恢复通用智能体能力，并实现极低的部署成本。摘要未提供具体数据集、指标、基线或数值增益。

Conclusion: 多阶段训练与OPD+CDD自蒸馏能够缓解领域知识注入和通用能力保持之间的冲突，使小参数角色扮演模型兼具高质量领域表现、较强通用能力和高效部署特性。

Abstract: This paper introduces the complete technical solution for the KuaiRP series of role-playing models. We aim to achieve four core objectives for a dedicated role-playing model: simplified prompt engineering, highly stable output quality, built-in domain world knowledge, and high-efficiency deployment with a small parameter size. However, effectively injecting deep domain knowledge often leads to a severe catastrophic forgetting of the model's general agent capabilities. To overcome this trade-off, we propose a multi-stage training pipeline. First, we design a standardized character template and construct an SFT data pipeline based on user behavior simulation and reverse profile filtering. Next, we utilize a rule-based composite reward function during the Reinforcement Learning (RL) phase to eliminate common degradation phenomena like length expansion and repetitive generation. Finally, to recover the general capabilities compromised during SFT and RL, we propose a novel self-distillation paradigm using Two-stage On-Policy Distillation (OPD) equipped with Cumulative-Divergence Decay (CDD). By using the domain-adapted model as the teacher and the original base model as the student, we effectively balance deep domain knowledge injection with the preservation of general agent capabilities. Experimental results demonstrate that the KuaiRP models not only match the current state-of-the-art proprietary models in role-playing fidelity within our target domains, but also successfully recover general agent capabilities, maintaining extremely low deployment costs.

</details>


### [139] [Same Day, Same Story; One Day Ahead, a Different Signal: The Dual Validity of Financial Sentiment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11144)
*AS Aravinthkakshan,Laven Srivastava,Harsh Nandwani*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文检验金融NLP中“人工标注一致性能够代表市场预测能力”的常见假设。基于证券集体诉讼语料、X平台消息与异常收益，比较五种情感工具，发现构念效度与预测效度的关系取决于抽样方式、预测时间窗口和分数表示；人工一致性可证明语义有效，但不足以决定预测排序。消息数量在含17.6%垃圾信息的对话中，既不能预测市场损失，也不能预测和解金额。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金融NLP通常先用人工标签验证情感工具，再将工具输出用于提取市场信号，但这隐含假设：语义/构念效度与预测效度衡量的是同一能力。本文旨在直接检验这一假设，并分析抽样规则、预测期限和情感分数表示方式是否会改变两类效度之间的关系。

Method: 构建2002—2025年证券集体诉讼语料，将70,500条X平台消息与异常股票收益相联系，并使用单一标注者建立人工标注金标准样本。对VADER、Loughran–McDonald、FinBERT、Twitter-RoBERTa和LLM标注器采用完全一致的处理流程进行比较。研究分别考察传统的工具特定抽样与固定样本量面板，并比较同日关联和提前一天预测，以及连续等级相关与粗粒度排序表现；同时检验消息数量对市场损失和和解金额的预测作用。

Result: 在工具特定抽样下，人工一致性与连续、同日的市场关联更一致，而不是与提前一天的预测关系一致。在固定样本量面板下，人工一致性在两个时间窗口的连续等级相关相近，但粗粒度排序仍然较弱。因此，抽样约定和分数表示会显著影响构念效度与预测效度的关系。另发现，消息量在垃圾信息占比17.6%的对话中，不能预测市场损失或和解金额。

Conclusion: 基准测试中的人工一致性主要证明工具具有语义或构念效度，不能单独证明其市场预测效度，更不能据此确定不同工具的预测排名。金融NLP评估应同时报告固定样本设计、时间窗口、连续分数关联和离散排序指标，并谨慎处理垃圾信息与消息量指标。

Abstract: Financial NLP has a standard workflow: validate a sentiment tool against human labels, then trust it to extract market signal. This assumes the two evaluations measure the same thing. We test that assumption in a setting where both can be measured at once: a corpus of securities class actions (2002-2025) linking 70,500 X messages to abnormal stock returns, with a single-annotator human labelled gold sample. Running five instruments (VADER, Loughran-McDonald, FinBERT, Twitter-RoBERTa, and an LLM annotator) through one identical pipeline, we find that the relationship between construct and predictive validity depends on the sampling convention and score representation. Under conventional method-specific sampling, human agreement aligns more closely with graded same-day associations than with one-day leads. On a fixed-n panel, however, agreement has similar graded rank correlations at both horizons, while the coarse ordering remains weak. Benchmark agreement therefore establishes semantic validity but does not by itself determine predictive rankings. In a conversation that is 17.6% spam, message volume predicts neither market damage nor settlement size.

</details>


### [140] [The Oligarch Barely Steers Model Collapse in Multi-Model Ecosystems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11146)
*Yangze Liu,Zhongyi Han*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究生成式人工智能市场集中度对递归训练导致的模型坍塌的影响。基于13个1–4B参数的开放模型构建多模型生态系统，并进行五代递归训练。结果显示，在测试范围内，市场份额不均不会显著改变坍塌速度或最终方向；真正决定坍塌速度的是哪些模型提供训练文本，以及这些模型文本被其他模型吸收的易感性。成员构成造成的速度差异可达2.8倍，易感性加权指标可解释R²=0.68的差异；加入约一半人类文本可使漂移大致减半。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有关于递归训练和模型坍塌的研究通常假设多个模型均分市场，但现实中的生成式人工智能市场更接近寡头竞争。本文希望检验两个问题：第一，市场集中度提高、有效数据来源减少且更同质化，是否会加速模型坍塌；第二，处于主导地位的模型是否会将后续模型牵引至其输出分布。

Method: 作者在受控生态系统中开展实验：使用13个参数规模为1–4B的开放模型，构建包含3至13个参与者的多模型生态系统，并加入一个探针模型，将最高市场份额推至90%。每一代中，各模型按市场份额向共享数据池贡献输出，随后所有模型均从干净的基础权重重新训练共享池数据，连续进行五代。实验比较市场份额不均、生态成员构成、模型易感性、注入偏置以及人类文本混入等因素对坍塌漂移和最终状态的影响。

Result: 市场份额更加集中几乎不改变坍塌速度，且不同份额分配和模型身份对五代后的终点影响仅为共同漂移的几个百分点，生态系统趋向近似相同的终点。即使最高份额达到极端水平并结合最强注入偏置，也不能稳定实现对其他模型的操控；产生的主题变化在坍塌度量中仅留下微弱痕迹。相比之下，在份额固定时，仅更换K=3生态系统的成员就会造成五代漂移速度达2.8倍差异。按市场份额加权的模型易感性指标能够以R²=0.68解释19组实验之间的速度差异。将约一半共享池替换为人类文本，可使漂移大致减半，但不改变其发展方向。

Conclusion: 在本文测试范围内，市场集中度既不是模型坍塌最终方向的主要决定因素，也不是其速度的主要决定因素。坍塌速度主要取决于哪些模型提供训练数据，以及这些模型的输出有多容易被生态系统中的其他模型吸收。人类数据能够显著减缓坍塌，但未改变其总体轨迹。结论应限定在所测试的模型规模、生态结构、五代训练过程和数据混合机制内，不能直接外推到所有真实市场或更大模型。

Abstract: AI-generated text is flowing back into the training corpora of the next generation of models. Recursive training on it drives model collapse, and recent work extends the setting to many models feeding one another -- but almost always with the market split evenly, while real generative AI is an oligopoly. Concentration raises two worries: fewer, more uniform sources may make collapse faster, and later models may be dragged toward the oligarch's output. We test both in controlled ecosystems: 13 open 1--4B models form natural ecosystems of 3 to 13 players, plus an injected probe that pushes the top share to 90%; each generation, every model's output is mixed into a shared pool by market share and every model is retrained on that pool from clean base weights, for five generations. Yet within the range we test, neither worry materializes; what emerges instead is an invariance. Making the split more unequal barely changes the speed of collapse. Destinations move even less: the share and identity knobs shift five-generation endpoints by only a few percent of the drift common to all arms -- the ecosystems collapse to nearly the same place.
